# 📝 Chapter 2: High-Yield Practice Questions (अभ्यास प्रश्न) — Expected RPSC Pattern

Practice Q1 🎯 Lok Devta & Sampradaya Deep-Dive
Which of the following folk deities of Rajasthan is worshiped as the deity of cattle protection who sacrificed his life fighting against the Meenas of Mer (मेर के मीणा) to rescue the cows of Lachha Gujari (लाछां गूजरी)?
(A) Devnarayanji (देवनारायणजी)
(B) Veer Tejaji (वीर तेजाजी)
(C) Mangleeya Mehaji (मांगलिया मेहाजी)
(D) Pabuji Rathore (पाबूजी)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Veer Tejaji (वीर तेजाजी)
Explanation: Tejaji protected the cows of Lachha Gujari from the Mers of Ajmer at Sursura / Sendariya. He was bitten by serpent Basak on his tongue.
Examiner Trap: Pabuji protected the cows of Deval Charni against Jind Rao Khichi of Jalore at Dechu (Jodhpur). Do not confuse Lachha Gujari (Tejaji) with Deval Charni (Pabuji).
Practice Q2 🎯 Sant Sampradaya Conceptual Pattern
Consider the following statements regarding the Dadu Panth (दादू पंथ):
1. Saint Dadu Dayal gave his spiritual sermons in 'Sadukkadi' or Dhundhari dialect.
2. The prayer hall of Dadu Panth is called 'Alakh Dariba' (अलख दरीबा).
3. The Naga branch of Dadu Panth was founded by disciple Saint Sundardas Ji (Junior).
4. Dadu Panthis traditionally cremate their dead immediately according to Vedic rituals.
Which of the statements given above are correct?
(A) 1, 2 and 3 only
(B) 1, 2, 3 and 4
(C) 2 and 4 only
(D) 1 and 3 only
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) 1, 2 and 3 only
Explanation: Statement 4 is incorrect because Dadu Panthis do NOT cremate or bury their dead. Instead, they leave the mortal remains in the open forest (like Bhairana hill) to serve as food for animals and birds. Dadu Dayal's body was placed at Bhairana hill in Naraina (Jaipur). Statements 1, 2, and 3 are historically authentic.
Practice Q3 🎯 Branches & Founders Matching
Match List-I (Ramsnehi Branch) with List-II (Founding Saint):
List-I (Branch)
(A) Shahpura (Bhilwara)
(B) Ren (Merta, Nagaur)
(C) Sinhthal (Bikaner)
(D) Khedapa (Jodhpur)
List-II (Founder)
(i) Sant Daryavji (संत दरियावजी)
(ii) Sant Ramcharanji (संत रामचरणजी)
(iii) Sant Ramdasji (संत रामदासजी)
(iv) Sant Hariramdasji (संत हरीरामदासजी)
(A) (A)-(iv), (B)-(i), (C)-(ii), (D)-(iii)
(B) (A)-(ii), (B)-(iii), (C)-(iv), (D)-(i)
(C) (A)-(ii), (B)-(i), (C)-(iv), (D)-(iii)
(D) (A)-(i), (B)-(ii), (C)-(iii), (D)-(iv)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) (A)-(ii), (B)-(i), (C)-(iv), (D)-(iii)
Explanation:
• Shahpura (Main Peeth) = Sant Ramcharanji (celebrates Phuldol festival).
• Ren (Nagaur) = Sant Daryavji (Ram = R for Raheem, M for Mohammed syncretism).
• Sinhthal (Bikaner) = Sant Hariramdasji (authored 'Nishani' yoga treatise).
• Khedapa (Jodhpur) = Sant Ramdasji.
Practice Q4 🎯 Lok Devi Iconography & History
Which folk goddess of Rajasthan is known as the goddess of Bees (मधुमक्खियों की देवी / भंवर माता) whose temple at Rewasa (Sikar) possesses the longest folk hymn (गीत) in Rajasthani literature, and whose shrine Aurangzeb's army failed to desecrate due to a swarm of divine wasps?
(A) Sakambhari Mata (शाकम्भरी माता)
(B) Karni Mata (करणी माता)
(C) Jeen Mata (जीण माता)
(D) Shila Devi (शीला देवी)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Jeen Mata (जीण माता)
Explanation: Jeen Mata's temple is on Kajla mountain in Rewasa (Sikar). Her brother Harsh Bhairav's temple is nearby. When Aurangzeb attacked, giant wasps repelled the imperial soldiers, leading Aurangzeb to pledge 1.25 mounds of oil for the akhand deepak. Her folk hymn (Chirja) is the longest in Rajasthani folklore.
Practice Q5 🎯 Environmental & Ecological Ethics
Saint Jambhoji founded the Bishnoi Sampradaya in 1485 CE at Samrathal Dhora (Bikaner) by laying down 29 tenets. Which of the following animals and trees are held most sacred by Bishnois?
(A) Lion (सिंह) and Banyan tree (बरगद)
(B) Camel (ऊंट) and Rohida tree (रोहिड़ा)
(C) Blackbuck (काला हिरण) and Khejri tree (खेजड़ी)
(D) Cow (गाय) and Peepal tree (पीपल)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Blackbuck (काला हिरण) and Khejri tree (खेजड़ी)
Explanation: Jambhoji considered Khejri (Prosopis cineraria) and blackbucks sacrosanct. This ecological consciousness led to the historic Khejarli massacre of 1730 CE where Amrita Devi Bishnoi and 363 Bishnois sacrificed their lives to protect green Khejri trees from the axe of Maharaja Abhai Singh's royal officials.

# 🌟 Comprehensive Topic-Wise Master Concept Questions (प्रत्येक विषय के गहन अभ्यास प्रश्न)

🎯 Lok Devis — Karni Mata & Deshnoke Rats (Kaba) — Deep Fact Check RPSC 1st Grade & EO/RO
At the world-famous marble temple of Karni Mata (Deshnoke, Bikaner), what are the thousands of sanctified White and Black Rats roaming freely and fed by devotees known as, and which community strictly abstains from bathing in Kolayat Lake due to the drowning of Karni Mata's foster son Lakhan?
(A) Rats called 'Kaba' (काबा); Charan community (चारण समाज)
(B) Rats called 'Mooshak'; Bishnoi community
(C) Rats called 'Sewak'; Gurjar community
(D) Rats called 'Bhomia'; Meena community
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Rats called 'Kaba' (काबा); Charan community (चारण समाज)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: The rats of Karni Mata are revered as **'Kaba' (काबा)** (believed to be reincarnated Charan devotees). Seeing a white Kaba brings supreme blessings. Because Karni Mata's adopted son Lakhan (Lakhana) drowned in Kolayat Lake on Kartik Purnima, the **Charan community never visits or bathes in Kolayat Lake**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B)? Mooshak is the general Sanskrit term for mouse, not the sacred Deshnoke name.
  • Why not (C)? Gurjars revere Devnarayanji and Kailadevi.
  • Why not (D)? Bhomiyas are martyr warrior deities.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Karni Mata rats = Kaba; Kolayat bath avoided by = Charan community; Child name of Karni Mata = Riddhi Bai; Foundation of Mehrangarh laid by = Karni Mata.
🎯 Lok Devis — Jin Mata of Rewasa (Sikar) & Deepest Chirja Hymns — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & 2nd Grade
The temple of Jin Mata (जीण माता, रेवासा - सीकर) on the Kajal Shikhar hill is famous for possessing the longest devotional folk song / hymn (सबसे लम्बा लोकगीत - 'Chirja' / चिरजा) in Rajasthani folklore. Which Mughal Emperor attempted to destroy this temple but fled when attacked by swarms of divine bees (Bhaunre)?
(A) Akbar
(B) Aurangzeb (औरंगजेब - who subsequently sent 1.25 maunds of pure ghee/oil annually from Delhi for the eternal lamp)
(C) Alauddin Khilji
(D) Jahangir
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Aurangzeb (औरंगजेब - who subsequently sent 1.25 maunds of pure ghee/oil annually from Delhi for the eternal lamp)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: When **Aurangzeb's Mughal army** attacked the Jin Mata temple at Rewasa (Sikar), millions of honeybees (*Bhaunre*) swarmed out of the sanctum, stinging the soldiers into retreat. A repentant Aurangzeb pledged to send **1.25 maunds (सव्वा मण) of pure oil/ghee from Delhi** to sustain the eternal flame (*Akhand Jyoti*), a royal custom continued for centuries. Jin Mata's devotional ballad (*Chirja*) is the longest song in Rajasthani folk literature (sung with *Damru* and *Chang* by her Jogi/Bhopa priests).

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Akbar? Akbar walked barefoot to Amer and Jwala Ji.
  • Why not (C) Alauddin? Alauddin destroyed temples at Chittorgarh, Jalore, and Ranthambore.
  • Why not (D) Jahangir? Jahangir gave royal grants to Pushkar.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Longest Rajasthani Folk Song = Jin Mata Chirja; Attack by bees on Aurangzeb = Jin Mata (Rewasa, Sikar); Brother's shrine = Harshnath (Bhairon).
🎯 Lok Devis — Kailadevi of Karauli & Languriya Songs — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & 1st Grade
At the Kailadevi Fair (कैलादेवी, करौली) on the banks of the Kalisil River during Chaitra Shukla Ashtami (Lakkhi Fair), what are the distinct folk songs and ecstatic devotional dances performed by Meena and Gurjar devotees in front of the Bohra Bhagat ki Chhatri called?
(A) Ghoomar and Panihari
(B) Chari and Terah Taali
(C) Languriya songs and Jogania / Kanak-Dandavat dance (लांगुरिया गीत एवं जोगणिया नृत्य)
(D) Gavari and Hathi Mana
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Languriya songs and Jogania / Kanak-Dandavat dance (लांगुरिया गीत एवं जोगणिया नृत्य)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Devotees of **Kailadevi (Karauli)** perform **Languriya (लांगुरिया)** songs and energetic dances honoring Lord Hanuman (*Languriya*). Devotees offer prostrations (*Kanak-Dandavat*) at the cenotaph of **Bohra Bhagat (बोहरा भगत की छतरी)** located right in front of the main temple complex to cure snakebites and childhood illnesses.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A)? *Panihari* and *Ghoomar* are general Marwari songs/dances.
  • Why not (B)? *Chari* is Gujjar dance of Kishangarh; *Terah Taali* is Kamad dance of Ramdevji.
  • Why not (D)? *Gavari* is Bhil tribal dance drama of Mewar.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Languriya songs & Bohra Bhagat Chhatri = Kailadevi (Karauli, Kalisil river); Fair = Chaitra Shukla 8 (Lakkhi Mela).
🎯 Lok Devis — Sheetla Mata & Chaksu Donkey Vehicle — Deep Fact Check RPSC 2nd Grade & EO/RO
At the sacred shrine of Sheetla Mata (सील की डूंगरी, चाकसू - जयपुर) built by Maharaja Madho Singh I, which priest community conducts the rituals, what is the Goddess's sacred vahana (vehicle), and what is the offering of cold food called?
(A) Brahmin priest; Lion vahana; Prashad
(B) Nayak priest; Camel vahana; Lapasi
(C) Meghwal priest; Horse vahana; Churma
(D) Kumhar (Potter / कुम्हार) priest; Donkey (गधा) vahana; Basoda (बासोड़ा - stale cold food)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Kumhar (Potter / कुम्हार) priest; Donkey (गधा) vahana; Basoda (बासोड़ा - stale cold food)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Sheetla Mata is the patron goddess of smallpox/measles (*Chetak/Bodari*). Her vehicle is the **Donkey (गधा)**, her priest is from the **Kumhar (Potter) caste**, and her idol is a broken sacred stone. On **Chaitra Krishna Ashtami (Sheetlashtami)**, no kitchen fires are lit, and families consume cold food cooked the previous evening called **Basoda (बासोड़ा)**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A)? Conventional temples have Brahmin priests and warm cooked Bhog.
  • Why not (B)? Nayak priests sing Pabuji's Phad.
  • Why not (C)? Meghwal priests are *Rikhiya* of Ramdevji.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Kumhar priest + Donkey vehicle + Cold stale food (Basoda) + Chaitra Krishna 8 = Sheetla Mata (Chaksu, Jaipur).
🎯 Lok Devis — Aai Mata of Bilara (Saffron Lamp Flame) — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & 1st Grade
At the main temple (called 'Bader' / बडेर) of Aai Mata (आई माता, बिलाड़ा - जोधपुर), patron goddess of the Sirvi agricultural community and disciple of Baba Ramdevji, what miraculous substance continuously drips from the sacred flame of the Akhand Jyoti?
(A) Saffron / Kesar (अखण्ड ज्योति की लौ से केसर / कस्तूरी टपकती है)
(B) Holy Sandalwood milk
(C) Pure Silver pearls
(D) Rose essence
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Saffron / Kesar (अखण्ड ज्योति की लौ से केसर / कस्तूरी टपकती है)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Aai Mata (incarnation of Goddess Hinglaj) founded the **Aai Panth with 11 rules** for the **Sirvi (सीरवी) community** at Bilara (Jodhpur). Her temples are called **'Bader' (बडेर)** and shrines are called **'Dargah' (दरगाह)**. In her sanctum, there is no stone idol, only an eternal flame from which pure **Saffron (केसर)** drops continuously.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B)? Sandalwood offerings are given at Jain shrines.
  • Why not (C)? Silver canopies (*Chhatra*) are installed in temples.
  • Why not (D)? Rose essence is famous from Pushkar/Khamnor.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Saffron dripping from lamp + Bader/Dargah temple + Sirvi Kuldevi = Aai Mata (Bilara, Jodhpur).
🎯 Sufi Saints — Khwaja Moinuddin Chishti & Urs of Ajmer — Deep Fact Check RPSC 1st Grade & College Lecturer
Khwaja Moinuddin Hasan Chishti (Garib Nawaz), who arrived in Ajmer during the reign of Prithviraj Chauhan III (1192 AD), was initiated into the Chishti order by which spiritual master in Sanjar (Persia)?
(A) Sheikh Hamiduddin Nagauri
(B) Khwaja Usman Harooni (ख्वाजा उस्मान हारूनी)
(C) Bakhtiyar Kaki
(D) Nizamuddin Auliya
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Khwaja Usman Harooni (ख्वाजा उस्मान हारूनी)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Khwaja Moinuddin Chishti was the direct disciple (*Murid*) of **Khwaja Usman Harooni**. He established the Chishti Sufi Silsila in India at Ajmer. His annual **Urs** is celebrated from **1st to 6th Rajab** at the Ajmer Dargah, inaugurated when the Gauri family of Bhilwara hoists the sacred flag on the Buland Darwaza.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Hamiduddin Nagauri? Sheikh Hamiduddin (*Sultan-ut-Tarikin* / King of Ascetics) was Khwaja's chief disciple who lived as a vegetarian farmer in Suwal, Nagaur.
  • Why not (C) Bakhtiyar Kaki? Qutbuddin Bakhtiyar Kaki was Khwaja's disciple in Delhi for whom the Qutb Minar was named.
  • Why not (D) Nizamuddin Auliya? Hazrat Nizamuddin was the master of Amir Khusrau in Delhi.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Master of Khwaja Chishti = Khwaja Usman Harooni; Urs dates = 1 to 6 Rajab; Flag hoisted by = Gauri Family (Bhilwara); Sultan-ut-Tarikin = Hamiduddin Nagauri.
🎯 Folk Deities — Veer Bigaji & Jakhar Community — Deep Fact Check RPSC 2nd Grade & SI
Which brave folk deity of Bikaner (born at Ridi village to Rao Mahant and Sultani Devi) sacrificed his life fighting Muslim cow-raiders at Rathjana to rescue the cows of a Brahmin woman, and is revered as the Kuldevta of the Jakhar (जाखड़) Jat community?
(A) Veer Panrajji (पनराजजी - Jaisalmer)
(B) Veer Fattaji (वीर फत्ताजी - Santhu, Jalore)
(C) Veer Bigaji (वीर बीगाजी - Bikaner/Rathjana)
(D) Bhomiyaji
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Veer Bigaji (वीर बीगाजी - Bikaner/Rathjana)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: **Veer Bigaji** was a warrior from Ridi (Dungargarh, Bikaner). He laid down his life fighting cattle raiders at **Rathjana** to protect a Brahmin's cows. He is worshipped as the supreme Kuldevta of the **Jakhar gotra Jats**. His annual fair is held on **14 October (Bhadrapad / Ashvin)** at Bigagaon.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Panrajji? Veer Panrajji protected cows of a Brahmin in Panrajasar (Jaisalmer).
  • Why not (B) Fattaji? Veer Fattaji fought invaders at Santhu village (Jalore) on Bhadrapad Shukla Navami.
  • Why not (D) Bhomiyaji? Bhomiyaji are local land/village martyr spirits.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Kuldevta of Jakhar Jats = Veer Bigaji (Rathjana, Bikaner); Santhu village martyr = Veer Fattaji (Jalore); Panrajasar martyr = Veer Panrajji (Jaisalmer).
🎯 Panch Pirs — Identification & Mnemonic — Deep Fact Check RPSC 2nd Grade & EO/RO
Which of the following celebrated folk deities of Rajasthan is NOT included among the holy 'Panch Pirs' (पंच पीर) revered equally by both Hindus and Muslims?
(A) Pabuji Rathore (पाबूजी)
(B) Baba Ramdevji (रामदेवजी)
(C) Veer Tejaji Maharaj (वीर तेजाजी)
(D) Gogaji Chauhan (गोगाजी)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Veer Tejaji Maharaj (वीर तेजाजी)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Veer Tejaji is a supremely revered folk deity of Rajasthan (born in Kharnal, Nagaur), but he is **NOT one of the Panch Pirs**. The 5 Panch Pirs are: **Pabuji, Harbhuji, Ramdevji, Mehaji Manglia, and Gogaji** (*'पाबू, हड़बू, रामदे, मांगलिया मेहा। पांचू पीर पधारज्ये, गोगाजी जेहा॥'*).

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Pabuji? Pabuji of Kolumand is one of the 5 Panch Pirs.
  • Why not (B) Ramdevji? Ramdevji of Runicha is one of the 5 Panch Pirs (worshipped as *Ramshah Pir* by Muslims).
  • Why not (D) Gogaji? Gogaji of Dadrewa is one of the 5 Panch Pirs (worshipped as *Jahir Pir*).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Panch Pirs = Pabuji, Harbhuji, Ramdevji, Mehaji, Gogaji. (Tejaji & Devnarayanji are NOT in Panch Pirs!).
🎯 Folk Deities — Harbhuji Sankhla & His Divine Bullock Cart — Deep Fact Check RPSC 1st Grade & RAS Pre
At the sacred temple of Harbhuji Sankhla (हड़बूजी सांखला) in Bengti (फलौदी/जोधपुर), what sanctified object is traditionally worshipped by devotees instead of a stone idol?
(A) His battle sword gifted by Balinathji
(B) His painted prayer scroll (Phad)
(C) His turban and silver sandals (खड़ाऊं)
(D) His miraculous wooden bullock cart (छकड़ा गाड़ी - used to fetch fodder for disabled cows)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) His miraculous wooden bullock cart (छकड़ा गाड़ी - used to fetch fodder for disabled cows)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Harbhuji (son of Maharaj Mehaji Sankhla of Bhundel, Nagaur and cousin of Ramdevji) devoted his life to serving crippled, sick, and disabled cows. In his temple at Bengti (built by Maharaja Ajit Singh of Jodhpur in 1721), his **miraculous wooden cart (छकड़ा गाड़ी)** is enshrined and worshipped. He also gifted a blessed dagger (*Katari*) to Rao Jodha to reclaim Mandore.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A)? Harbhuji gifted a blessed sword to Rao Jodha.
  • Why not (B)? Phads are worshipped for Pabuji and Devnarayanji.
  • Why not (C)? Sandals (*Paglya*) are worshipped for Ramdevji, Jasnathji, and Dadu Dayal.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Harbhuji temple at Bengti = Worshipped through wooden bullock cart (छकड़ा गाड़ी); Temple built by Maharaja Ajit Singh.
🎯 Folk Deities — Kallaji Rathore 'Four-Handed Deity' — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & 2nd Grade
During the 3rd Saka of Chittorgarh (1568 AD) against Mughal Emperor Akbar, which Rathore warrior earned the immortal title of 'Four-Handed Deity' (चार हाथों वाले लोक देवता) after lifting his wounded uncle Jaimal onto his shoulders in combat?
(A) Veer Kallaji Rathore (वीर कल्लाजी राठौड़)
(B) Patta Chundawat (पत्ता चूंडावत)
(C) Achaldas Khinchi
(D) Durgadas Rathore
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Veer Kallaji Rathore (वीर कल्लाजी राठौड़)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: When **Jaimal Rathore** was incapacitated by Akbar's musket *Sangram*, his nephew **Kallaji Rathore** hoisted Jaimal onto his shoulders. Both warriors wielded two swords each, cutting through the Mughal lines with 4 flashing blades, causing spectators to hail Kallaji as the **'Four-Handed God' (चार हाथों वाले देवता / शेषनाग का अवतार)**. Kallaji's cenotaph is between Bhairon Pol and Hanuman Pol at Chittorgarh.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B) Patta Chundawat? Patta of Kelwa was the supreme commander of Chittorgarh whose wife Phool Kanwar led the 1568 Jauhar.
  • Why not (C) Achaldas Khinchi? Achaldas was the king of Gagron who sacrificed his life in the 1423 Saka against Hoshang Shah.
  • Why not (D) Durgadas Rathore? Durgadas fought the 30-Year War (1679–1709) for Maharaja Ajit Singh.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Four-Handed Deity & Sheshnag Avatar = Kallaji Rathore; Chhatri = Between Bhairon Pol & Hanuman Pol in Chittor.
🎯 Folk Deities — Tallinathji & Oran Conservation — Deep Fact Check RPSC EO/RO & 1st Grade
Which folk deity of Marwar (Shergarh/Jalore), whose real name was Gangdev Rathore and disciple of Guru Jalandharnath, is revered as the patron deity of sacred ecological groves called 'Oran' (ओरण - protected village forest land)?
(A) Mallinathji (मल्लीनाथजी - Barmer/Tilwara)
(B) Tallinathji (तल्लीनाथजी - Panchmukha Hill, Gadoi, Jalore)
(C) Devbaba (देवबाबा - Nagla Jahaz, Bharatpur)
(D) Khetarpal (क्षेत्रपाल - Agricultural Guardian)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Tallinathji (तल्लीनाथजी - Panchmukha Hill, Gadoi, Jalore)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Tallinathji (तल्लीनाथजी) (real name Gangdev Rathore, son of Viramdev of Shergarh) established his spiritual seat on **Panchmukha Hill at Gadoi (Jalore)**. He strictly prohibited cutting any green trees in designated forest tracts called **'Oran' (ओरण)**, making him Rajasthan's pioneer deity of sacred forest conservation.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Mallinathji? Mallinathji ruled Malani (Barmer), established the *Kunda Panth* (1399), and is worshipped at Tilwara on the Luni river.
  • Why not (C) Devbaba? Devbaba is the veterinarian and cattle-healer deity of Nagla Jahaz (Bharatpur).
  • Why not (D) Khetarpal? Khetarpal is the general village territory protector deity.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Tallinathji = Oran / Tree conservation + Panchmukha Hill (Jalore); Mallinathji = Tilwara (Barmer) + Kunda Panth.
🎯 Folk Deities — Devbaba of Nagla Jahaz (Bharatpur) — Deep Fact Check RPSC 2nd Grade & SI
Which folk deity of eastern Rajasthan (Bharatpur) is revered especially by the Gurjar and Meena communities as the Supreme Physician of Cattle & Animals (पशु चिकित्सा के ज्ञाता / ग्वालों के देवता), worshipped with offerings of Lapasi and Jhadu (लापसी एवं झाडू)?
(A) Mama Dev (मामा देव - Rain God)
(B) Iloji (इलोजी - Bachelor God of Barmer)
(C) Devbaba of Nagla Jahaz (देवबाबा, नगला जहाज - भरतपुर)
(D) Jhunjharji (झूंझारजी - Sikar)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Devbaba of Nagla Jahaz (देवबाबा, नगला जहाज - भरतपुर)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: **Devbaba** has his main shrine at **Nagla Jahaz (Bharatpur)**. He possessed supernatural knowledge of veterinary herbs (*Ayurvedic animal healing*). Devotees feast seven cowherds (*Gwalas*) and offer sweet porridge (*Lapasi*) and brooms (*Jhadu*) at his semi-annual fairs held on **Bhadrapad Shukla Panchami and Chaitra Shukla Panchami**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Mama Dev? Mama Dev is the Rain God whose wooden Toran is installed outside village gates.
  • Why not (B) Iloji? Iloji is the deity of humor and fertility in Barmer.
  • Why not (D) Jhunjharji? Jhunjharji is the protector martyr deity of Imloha/Syalodara (Sikar).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Cattle Veterinarian deity = Devbaba (Nagla Jahaz, Bharatpur); Rain deity = Mama Dev (Wooden Toran); Bachelor/Fertility deity = Iloji.
🎯 Bhakti Saints — Dadu Dayal & 52 Pillars (Stambha) — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & RAS Pre
In the Dadu Panth (दादू पंथ) founded by Sant Dadu Dayal ('Kabir of Rajasthan'), what are the 52 principal disciples who established ashrams across India to propagate his teachings known as?
(A) 52 Dhwajadhari
(B) 52 Mahants
(C) 52 Pir
(D) 52 Stambha / Thambas (52 स्तम्भ या थांबे)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) 52 Stambha / Thambas (52 स्तम्भ या थांबे)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Sant Dadu Dayal (1544–1603 AD) had 152 total disciples, of whom 100 were free ascetics (*Vitaragi*) and **52 chief disciples were appointed as heads of branch monasteries**, famously designated as the **'52 Stambha' (52 स्तम्भ)** of the sect. The main seat is at **Naraina (Jaipur)**, and prayer grounds are called *Alakh Dariba*.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A)? *Dhwajadhari* is not a formal Dadu Panth designation.
  • Why not (B)? *Mahants* are general heads of Hindu maths.
  • Why not (C)? *Pirs* are Sufi/folk masters.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: 52 Stambha = Dadu Panth (Dadu Dayal); Meeting with Akbar in 1585 at Fatehpur Sikri = Dadu Dayal; Discourses = Dhundhari (Sadhu-bhasha).
🎯 Bhakti Saints — Sant Rajjabji (The Groom Saint) — Deep Fact Check RPSC 1st Grade & EO/RO
Which famous disciple of Sant Dadu Dayal, upon hearing Dadu's single couplet at Amer while proceeding to his wedding, cancelled his marriage and remained dressed in the attire of a Bridegroom (दूल्हे के वेश में) for his entire remaining life while authoring 'Sarvangi' (सर्वंगी)?
(A) Sant Rajjabji of Sanganer (सन्त रज्जब जी, सांगानेर)
(B) Sant Sundardas (संत सुन्दरदास)
(C) Sant Daryavji
(D) Sant Ramdasji
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Sant Rajjabji of Sanganer (सन्त रज्जब जी, सांगानेर)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Pathan nobleman **Sant Rajjabji** of Sanganer was riding a mare in groom attire to Amer for his wedding when he heard Dadu's verse: 'रज्जब तैं गजब कियौ, सिर पर बांध्यौ मोड़। आया था हरि भजन कौ, करै नरक की दौड़॥'. Rajjab immediately abandoned his marriage, remained in groom clothes for life, preserved Dadu's verses in *'Dadu Dayal ri Vani'*, and authored his own spiritual masterpiece **'Sarvangi'**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B) Sundardas? Sant Sundardas (the greatest scholar-poet of Dadu Panth from Dausa) founded the warrior ascetic *Naga Branch* and authored *Sundar Vilas*.
  • Why not (C) Daryavji? Sant Daryavji founded the Ren (Nagaur) seat of the Ramsnehi sect.
  • Why not (D) Ramdasji? Sant Ramdasji founded the Kherapa (Jodhpur) seat of the Ramsnehi sect.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Groom dress for life + 'Sarvangi' = Sant Rajjabji (Sanganer); Naga Branch + 'Sundar Vilas' = Sant Sundardas (Dausa).
🎯 Bhakti Saints — Sant Charan Das & Nadir Shah's Invasion — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & 1st Grade
Which saint of Rajasthan (born at Dehra, Alwar), who formulated 42 moral rules for his disciples and established his main seat in Delhi, accurately predicted the devastating invasion of Delhi by Persian Emperor Nadir Shah (1739 AD) years before it happened?
(A) Sant Lal Das (सन्त लालदास)
(B) Sant Charan Das (सन्त चरणदास जी)
(C) Sant Pipa (सन्त पीपा)
(D) Sant Dhanna (सन्त धन्ना)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Sant Charan Das (सन्त चरणदास जी)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: **Sant Charan Das** (1703–1782 AD) founded the Charan Dasi sect with **42 strict rules**. He foretold Mughal Emperor Muhammad Shah Rangeela about the catastrophic sack of Delhi by **Nadir Shah in 1739**. His prominent scholarly female disciples were **Sahjo Bai** (author of *Sahaj Prakash*) and **Daya Bai** (author of *Daya Bodh* and *Vinay Malika*).

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Lal Das? Sant Lal Das of Dholiduv (Alwar) founded the Lal Dasi sect emphasizing Hindu-Muslim unity and self-earned livelihood.
  • Why not (C) Pipa? Sant Pipa was the Khinchi ruler of Gagron who became a tailor and disciple of Ramananda.
  • Why not (D) Dhanna? Sant Dhanna was the Jat saint of Dhuan Kalan (Tonk).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: 42 Rules + Predicted Nadir Shah's 1739 invasion + Disciples Sahjo Bai & Daya Bai = Sant Charan Das.
🎯 Bhakti Saints — Sant Pipa of Gagron — Deep Fact Check RPSC 2nd Grade & SI
Sant Pipa (real name Pratap Singh Khinchi), who abdicated the royal throne of Gagron Fort to become a disciple of Swami Ramananda and took up the profession of tailoring, is revered as the patron deity of which community in Rajasthan?
(A) Potter (Kumhar) Community
(B) Blacksmith (Lohar) Community
(C) Tailor (Darzi) Community (दर्जी समुदाय)
(D) Carpenter (Suthar) Community
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Tailor (Darzi) Community (दर्जी समुदाय)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: King Pratap Singh of Gagron renounced his wealth and wives under the guidance of **Swami Ramananda** in Varanasi, adopted the name **Sant Pipa**, and earned his livelihood through stitching clothes. He is worshipped by the **Darzi (Tailor) community**. His temple is at **Samdari (Balotra/Barmer)**, his cave is at **Toda Rai Singh (Tonk)**, and his cenotaph (*Chhatri*) is at **Gagron Fort (Jhalawar)**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Kumhars? Kumhars revere *Sheetla Mata* and *Molela Terracotta* traditions.
  • Why not (B) Lohars? Lohars revere *Lord Vishwakarma*.
  • Why not (D) Suthars? Suthars of Bassi revere *Lord Vishwakarma* and pioneer *Kavad* woodwork.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Sant Pipa: Patron deity of Tailor (Darzi) community; Temple = Samdari; Cave = Toda Rai Singh; Chhatri = Gagron Fort.
🎯 Bhakti Saints — Sant Mavji & Beneshwar Dham — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & RAS Pre
At the holy confluence of the Som, Mahi, and Jakham rivers (Beneshwar Dham, Dungarpur), which saint founded the Nishkalank Sampradaya (निष्कलंक सम्प्रदाय) and authored 5 massive prophetic hand-written volumes titled 'Chopada' (चोपड़ा)?
(A) Sant Dadu Dayal
(B) Sant Jasnathji
(C) Sant Ramcharanji
(D) Sant Mavji (संत मावजी)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Sant Mavji (संत मावजी)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: **Sant Mavji** (born 1714 AD at Sabla, Dungarpur) is revered by Bhils as the 10th incarnation of Lord Vishnu (**Kalki Avatar**). He established the pilgrim center **Beneshwar Dham** (*'Kumbh of the Tribals'*) on the Som-Mahi-Jakham Triveni. He penned 5 prophetic texts called **'Chopada'** in the local Vagadi dialect (containing 3.5 crore words and illustrations of future global events).

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Dadu Dayal? Dadu Dayal founded the Dadu Panth at Naraina (Jaipur).
  • Why not (B) Jasnathji? Sant Jasnathji founded the Jasnathi sect at Katariasar (Bikaner).
  • Why not (C) Ramcharanji? Sant Ramcharanji founded the Shahpura branch of the Ramsnehi sect.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Beneshwar Dham + Nishkalank Sampradaya + 'Chopada' prophetic texts = Sant Mavji; Broken Shivling worshipped at = Beneshwar Dham.
🎯 Folk Deities — Pabuji (Birth, Mare & Deval Charani) — Deep Fact Check RPSC EO/RO & 1st Grade
Which black mare was gifted to Pabuji Rathore (पाबूजी) by Charani woman Deval Charani (देवल चारणी), on the solemn condition that Pabuji would defend her cows from cattle raiders at any personal cost?
(A) Leelan / Shringar (लीलण - Tejaji's mare)
(B) Kesar Kalmi (केसर कालमी - Pabuji's black mare)
(C) Kiran Kabra (किरण काबरा - Mehaji Manglia's horse)
(D) Leelagar (लीलागर - Devnarayanji's horse)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Kesar Kalmi (केसर कालमी - Pabuji's black mare)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Deval Charani gifted her prized black mare **Kesar Kalmi (केसर कालमी)** to Pabuji of Kolumand. When his brother-in-law Jindraj Khichi of Nagaur raided Deval's cows, Pabuji left his wedding ceremony midway (*Chauri pheras*) and sacrificed his life in the **Battle of Dhechu (1276 AD)** to protect the cattle.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Leelan? *Leelan (Shringar)* was the famous mare of **Tejaji Maharaj**, who carried the news of Tejaji's martyrdom back to his wife Pemalde.
  • Why not (C) Kiran Kabra? *Kiran Kabra* was the warhorse of Panch Pir **Mehaji Manglia** of Bapini (Jodhpur).
  • Why not (D) Leelagar? *Leelagar* was the divine stallion of **Devnarayanji (Gurjar deity of Asind)**.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Pabuji's mare = Kesar Kalmi; Tejaji's mare = Leelan; Mehaji's horse = Kiran Kabra; Devnarayanji's horse = Leelagar; Ramdevji's horse = Leela Ghoda.
🎯 Folk Deities — Pabuji (Camel Deity & Phad Reading) — Deep Fact Check RPSC 2nd Grade & SI
Who are the traditional pastoralists who revere Pabuji as the pioneer deity who first brought camels (सांडे / ऊंट) to Rajasthan, and which instrument is played by Nayak Bhopas during the recitation of Pabuji ki Phad?
(A) Bhil community; instrument: Mandal
(B) Gurjar community; instrument: Jantar
(C) Rebari / Raika community; instrument: Ravanhattha (रावणहत्था)
(D) Kamad community; instrument: Manjira
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Rebari / Raika community; instrument: Ravanhattha (रावणहत्था)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: The camel-rearing **Rebari / Raika (रेबारी / राइका)** community regards Pabuji as their chief protector deity. The Phad of Pabuji is recited by **Nayak / Thori Bhopas** using the 9-stringed bowed coconut shell instrument **Ravanhattha (रावणहत्था)**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A)? Bhils play the *Mandal* (clay drum) during Gavari dance.
  • Why not (B)? Gurjar Bhopas recite the Phad of **Devnarayanji** using the **Jantar (जन्तर)** instrument.
  • Why not (D)? Kamad women play 13 *Manjiras* while performing Terah Taali in devotion to **Baba Ramdevji**.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Pabuji = Rebari/Raika + Ravanhattha; Devnarayanji = Gurjar + Jantar; Ramdevji = Kamad + Terah Taali.
🎯 Folk Deities — Gogaji (Birth, Jahir Pir & Goga Navami) — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & RAS Pre
Which Turkish invader conferred the divine title of 'Jahir Pir' (जाहिर पीर - Living Manifest Saint) upon Gogaji Chauhan after witnessing his supernatural battle prowess, and on which tithi is Goga Navami celebrated?
(A) Muhammad Ghori; Tithi: Shravan Shukla Navami
(B) Emperor Akbar; Tithi: Ashvin Shukla Navami
(C) Alauddin Khilji; Tithi: Chaitra Shukla Navami
(D) Mahmud Ghaznavi; Tithi: Bhadrapada Krishna Navami (भाद्रपद कृष्ण नवमी)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Mahmud Ghaznavi; Tithi: Bhadrapada Krishna Navami (भाद्रपद कृष्ण नवमी)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: When Sultan Mahmud of Ghazni invaded northern Rajasthan, Gogaji (born at Dadrewa, Churu to Jewar and Bachhal) fought heroically with his 47 sons. Stunned by Gogaji's valor appearing simultaneously across the battlefield, Ghaznavi exclaimed 'Zahir Pir' (Manifest Deity). Goga Navami is celebrated on **Bhadrapada Krishna Navami** across North India under Khejdi trees (*गांव-गांव खेजड़ी, गांव-गांव गोगो*).

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A)? Muhammad Ghori fought Prithviraj Chauhan III in the Battles of Tarain (1191 & 1192 AD).
  • Why not (B)? Akbar ruled 500 years later during the 16th century.
  • Why not (C)? Chaitra Shukla Navami is Ram Navami (birth of Lord Rama).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Gogaji titled Jahir Pir by = Mahmud Ghaznavi; Tithi = Bhadrapad Krishna 9 (Goga Navami); Head shrine = Shirshmedi (Dadrewa); Body shrine = Gogamedi (Nohar).
🎯 Folk Deities — Gogaji (Architecture of Gogamedi) — Deep Fact Check RPSC 1st Grade & EO/RO
The sanctum sanctorum of Gogamedi / Dhurmedi (धुरमेड़ी, नोहर - हनुमानगढ़) was built in the architectural shape of a Mosque with 'Bismillah' (बिस्मिल्लाह) inscribed on its main gate by which Delhi Sultan, before being renovated into a temple by Maharaja Ganga Singh?
(A) Firoz Shah Tughlaq (फ़िरोज़ शाह तुग़लक)
(B) Alauddin Khilji
(C) Iltutmish
(D) Sikandar Lodi
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Firoz Shah Tughlaq (फ़िरोज़ शाह तुग़लक)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Delhi Sultan **Firoz Shah Tughlaq** constructed the tomb-like shrine of Gogaji at Nohar (Hanumangarh) in Islamic maqbara/mosque style and inscribed the holy Arabic phrase *'Bismillah'* above the entrance gate. In the 20th century, **Maharaja Ganga Singh of Bikaner** reconstructed the present grand temple surrounding it.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B) Alauddin Khilji? Alauddin Khilji built the *Alai Darwaza* in Delhi and the *Topkhana Mosque* at Jalore Fort.
  • Why not (C) Iltutmish? Iltutmish completed the Qutub Minar and granted lands in Nagaur to Sufi saint Hamiduddin Nagauri.
  • Why not (D) Sikandar Lodi? Sikandar Lodi was contemporary to Sant Jambhoji and Sant Jasnathji, granting land at Katariasar.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Gogamedi Mosque shape & 'Bismillah' = Built by Firoz Shah Tughlaq; Modern Temple façade = Maharaja Ganga Singh.
🎯 Folk Deities — Ramdevji (Birth, Dalit Sister Dalibai & Rituals) — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & EO/RO
Match the unique terms associated with the cult of Baba Ramdevji (रामदेवजी) with their correct descriptions:

Which of the following pairings is INCORRECT?
(A) Neja (नेजा) — Five-colored holy banner of Ramdevji
(B) Chopada (चोपड़ा) — Devotional poetic book composed by Ramdevji
(C) Jamma (जम्मा) — Night-long devotional hymn vigil (रात्रि जागरण)
(D) Rikhia (रिखिया) — Meghwal Dalit devotees of Ramdevji
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Chopada (चोपड़ा) — Devotional poetic book composed by Ramdevji
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Chopada (चोपड़ा) was authored by **Sant Mavji of Beneshwar**, containing 5 prophetic volumes. The devotional poetic work authored by Baba Ramdevji is **'Chaubees Vaniyan' (चौबीस वाणियां)**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Neja? *Neja* is the sacred 5-colored flag hoisted by devotees during the Ramdevra fair.
  • Why not (C) Jamma? *Jamma Jagaran* is the sacred all-night musical vigil organized by devotees of Ramdevji.
  • Why not (D) Rikhia? *Rikhia* is the traditional honorific term for Meghwal community disciples of Ramdevji, led historically by his adopted sister Sant Dalibai.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Ramdevji's poem = Chaubees Vaniyan; Sant Mavji's prophecy = Chopada; Ramdevji's flag = Neja; Meghwal devotees = Rikhia.
🎯 Folk Deities — Tejaji (Lachha Gujari & Parbatsar Fair) — Deep Fact Check RPSC 2nd Grade & Patwari
At which location did the serpent Basak (बाषक नाग) bite Veer Tejaji (वीर तेजाजी) on his tongue in keeping with his unbroken vow of truth, after Tejaji successfully rescued the cows of Lachha Gujari from the Meenas of Mer?
(A) Kharnal (खरनाल - Birthplace in Nagaur)
(B) Sursura (सुरसुरा, किशनगढ़ - Spot where Tejaji breathed his last)
(C) Sendariya (सेंदरिया, ब्यावर/अजमेर - Snake-bite spot)
(D) Parbatsar (परबतसर - Venue of grand cattle fair)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Sendariya (सेंदरिया, ब्यावर/अजमेर - Snake-bite spot)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: While marching to rescue Lachha Gujari's cows, Tejaji promised serpent Basak that he would return after fulfilling his duty. Upon victory, when whole body was wounded, he offered his uninjured tongue at **Sendariya (सेंदरिया)** for the snakebite. He breathed his last at **Sursura (सुरसुरा)** near Kishangarh.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Kharnal? Kharnal (Nagaur) is Tejaji's **birthplace (जन्मस्थली)** where his ancestral seat is located.
  • Why not (B) Sursura? Sursura is the place of **martyrdom (वीरगति स्थल)** where his mare Leelan brought the news of his death to his wife Pemalde.
  • Why not (D) Parbatsar? Parbatsar (Nagaur) is the site of the massive annual **Veer Teja Cattle Fair** held on Bhadrapad Shukla Dashami (*Teja Dashami*).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Tejaji: Birth = Kharnal; Snake bite = Sendariya; Demise = Sursura; Cattle Fair = Parbatsar; Cow owner = Lachha Gujari.
🎯 Folk Deities — Devnarayanji (Jantar Phad & Vishnu Avatar) — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & 1st Grade
Why is the sacred Phad of Devnarayanji (देवनारायण जी की फड़) unique in the folk culture of Rajasthan, and on which date did the Indian Postal Department issue a special commemorative postage stamp featuring it?
(A) It is painted on silk; postage stamp issued in 1947
(B) It is performed exclusively by women dancers
(C) It is recited without any musical instrument; stamp issued in 2020
(D) It is the longest, oldest, and most detailed Phad in Rajasthan (recited with Jantar by Gurjar Bhopas); commemorative postage stamp of ₹5 issued in 1992
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) It is the longest, oldest, and most detailed Phad in Rajasthan (recited with Jantar by Gurjar Bhopas); commemorative postage stamp of ₹5 issued in 1992
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Devnarayanji (incarnation of Lord Vishnu born at Asind to Sawai Bhoj and Sedhu Khatani) has Rajasthan's **longest and most intricate Phad**. Gurjar Bhopas sing it using the **Jantar (जन्तर)** instrument. In **1992**, the Government of India issued a 5-rupee postage stamp depicting Devnarayanji's Phad (and in 2011 on Devnarayanji himself). Devotees worship bricks with Neem leaves.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A)? Phads are traditionally painted on heavy hand-woven Khadi/canvas (*Reza* cloth) using vegetable dyes, never imported silk.
  • Why not (B)? Male Gurjar Bhopas sing the ballad.
  • Why not (C)? Devnarayanji's Phad relies on the resonant Jantar; Pabuji's Phad uses Ravanhattha.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Devnarayanji Phad = Longest Phad + Jantar instrument + 1992 Postage Stamp; Pabuji Phad = Most popular + Ravanhattha.
🎯 Environmental Saints — Jambhoji (29 Rules & Khejarli 1730) — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & EO/RO
During the reign of which Marwar Maharaja did the immortal Khejarli Massacre (खेजड़ली बलिदान - 1730 AD / VS 1787) occur, where Amrita Devi Vishnoi and 363 men and women sacrificed their lives hugging Khejri trees?
(A) Maharaja Abhay Singh (महाराजा अभय सिंह - Marwar)
(B) Maharaja Jaswant Singh I
(C) Maharaja Man Singh
(D) Maharaja Takht Singh
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Maharaja Abhay Singh (महाराजा अभय सिंह - Marwar)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: In 1730 AD (Bhadrapad Shukla Dashami), royal minister Girdhar Das Bhandari under **Maharaja Abhay Singh of Jodhpur** ordered the felling of green Khejri trees in Khejarli village for royal palace lime kilns. Amrita Devi, her 3 daughters, and 363 Vishnois sacrificed their heads (*'सिर साठे रूंख रहे तो भी सस्तो जाण'*). The world's only **Tree Fair (वृक्ष मेला)** is held here on Bhadrapad Shukla Dashami.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B) Jaswant Singh I? Maharaja Jaswant Singh I ruled earlier (1638–1678) during the Mughal war of succession (Battle of Dharmat 1658).
  • Why not (C) Man Singh? Maharaja Man Singh (1803–1843) was the patron of the Nath Sect who built the Maha Mandir in Jodhpur.
  • Why not (D) Takht Singh? Maharaja Takht Singh was the ruler of Jodhpur during the **1857 Revolt**.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Khejarli Sacrifice (1730 AD) = Ruler: Maharaja Abhay Singh; Leader: Amrita Devi; Martyrs: 363; Tithi: Bhadrapad Shukla 10 (Tree Fair).
🎯 Bhakti Saints — Jasnathji (36 Rules & Agni Dance) — Deep Fact Check RPSC 2nd Grade & SI
Which holy war-cry is chanted by the Siddh disciples of Sant Jasnathji as they enter trance-like state and dance bare-foot over glowing live charcoal embers in the Agni Dance (अग्नि नृत्य) at Katariasar (Bikaner)?
(A) 'Har Har Mahadev' (हर-हर महादेव)
(B) 'Fateh-Fateh' (फ़तेह-फ़तेह!)
(C) 'Jai Gopinathji' (जय गोपीनाथ जी)
(D) 'Bolo Tejaji Maharaj ki Jai'
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) 'Fateh-Fateh' (फ़तेह-फ़तेह!)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: The Jasnathi Siddhs of Katariasar (Bikaner) perform the thrilling **Agni Nritya (अग्नि नृत्य)** atop glowing red-hot charcoal embers (*Dhoona*). They enter the fire pit chanting the ecstatic spiritual invocation **'Fateh-Fateh' (फ़तेह-फ़तेह!)** and perform agricultural actions like churning milk and grinding grain (*Matira fodna*) without sustaining any burns.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Har Har Mahadev? *Har Har Mahadev* is the Shaivite battle cry used by Rajput warriors.
  • Why not (C) Jai Gopinathji? *Gopinathji* is a Vaishnava deity of Jaipur.
  • Why not (D)? *Tejaji's name* is invoked by farmers while sowing crops.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Agni Dance = Katariasar (Bikaner) + Jasnathi Siddhs + 'Fateh-Fateh!' chant; Vishnoi = 29 Rules (Jambhoji); Jasnathi = 36 Rules (Jasnathji).
🎯 Bhakti Saints — Ramsnehi Sect (4 Branches & Founders) — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & RAS Pre
Match the 4 principal monastic seats (पीठें) of the Ramsnehi Sampradaya (रामस्नेही सम्प्रदाय) with their respective founder saints:

Which of the following pairs is INCORRECT?
(A) Shahpura Main Seat (शाहपुरा, भीलवाड़ा) — Sant Ramcharanji (Phool Dol fair)
(B) Ren Branch (रेण, मेड़ता - नागौर) — Sant Daryavji
(C) Kherapa Branch (खेड़ापा, जोधपुर) — Sant Mavji
(D) Sinthal Branch (सिंहथल, बीकानेर) — Sant Hariramdasji (Author of 'Nishani')
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Kherapa Branch (खेड़ापा, जोधपुर) — Sant Mavji
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: The **Kherapa (Jodhpur)** seat of Ramsnehi Sampradaya was founded by **Sant Ramdasji (संत रामदास जी)** (disciple of Sant Hariramdasji), NOT Sant Mavji. Sant Mavji founded the **Nishkalank Sampradaya** at Beneshwar (Dungarpur).

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Shahpura? Sant Ramcharanji founded the main Shahpura seat, celebrating the famous **Phool Dol Mahotsav (फूलडोल महोत्सव)** from Chaitra Krishna Pratipada to Panchami.
  • Why not (B) Ren? Sant Daryavji (born Jaitaran) founded the Ren seat in Nagaur, harmonizing 'Ra' (Ram) and 'Ma' (Muhammad).
  • Why not (D) Sinthal? Sant Hariramdasji founded the Sinthal seat in Bikaner and authored the iconic yoga-bhakti text *'Nishani'* (निशानी).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Ramsnehi 4 Seats: Shahpura = Ramcharanji; Ren = Daryavji; Sinthal = Hariramdasji ('Nishani'); Kherapa = Ramdasji.
🎯 Panch Pirs — Ramdevji & Social Reforms — Deep Concept Check RPSC EO/RO & Patwari
Which folk deity of Rajasthan is uniquely celebrated as a divine social reformer who founded the Kamadiya Panth (कामुड़िया पंथ) and authored the devotional poem collection 'Chaubees Vaniyan' (चौबीस वाणियां)?
(A) Pabuji (पाबूजी महाराज)
(B) Gogaji (गोगाजी चौहान)
(C) Mehaji Manglia (मेहाजी मांगलिया)
(D) Ramdevji (रामदेवजी तंवर)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Ramdevji (रामदेवजी तंवर)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Baba Ramdevji (Tanwar Rajput, born Undu-Kashmir, Barmer) established the egalitarian Kamadiya Panth, adopted Dalit devotee Dalibai as his sister, and composed 'Chaubees Vaniyan' (he is the only Lok Devta who was also a celebrated poet). Female disciples perform the Terah Taali dance in his honor.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Pabuji? Pabuji (Rathore of Kolumand) is revered as the God of Camels and Plagues who defended Deval Charani's cows; his Phad is recited by Nayak Bhopas using the Ravanhattha.
  • Why not (B) Gogaji? Gogaji (Chauhan of Dadrewa) fought Mahmud Ghaznavi and is worshipped as Jahir Pir across northern India under Khejdi trees on Goga Navami.
  • Why not (C) Mehaji Manglia? Mehaji Manglia (Panch Pir) had his main shrine at Bapini (Jodhpur); his horse was named *Kiran Kabra* and his fair falls on Krishna Janmashtami.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Poet Lok Devta + Kamadiya Panth = Ramdevji; God of Camels = Pabuji; Jahir Pir = Gogaji; Kiran Kabra horse = Mehaji Manglia.
🎯 Environmental Saints — Jambhoji & Vishnoi Commandments — Deep Concept Check RPSC RAS Pre & 1st Grade
In 1485 AD at Samrathal Dhora (Bikaner), Sant Jambhoji (जाम्भोजी) laid down 29 strict religious and ecological commandments that led to the founding of which sacred sect?
(A) Jasnathi Sect (जसनाथी सम्प्रदाय - 36 Rules)
(B) Vishnoi Sect (बिश्नोई सम्प्रदाय - 29 Rules)
(C) Ramsnehi Sect (रामस्नेही सम्प्रदाय)
(D) Nishkalank Sect (निष्कलंक सम्प्रदाय)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Vishnoi Sect (बिश्नोई सम्प्रदाय - 29 Rules)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Sant Jambhoji founded the Vishnoi Sect (Bish = 20 + Noi = 9) in 1485 AD at Samrathal Dhora. The 29 commandments strictly prohibit cutting green trees (especially Khejdi) and killing animals, culminating in the historic 1730 AD Khejarli sacrifice where Amrita Devi and 363 martyrs laid down their lives.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Jasnathi Sect? The Jasnathi sect was founded at Katariasar (Bikaner) by Sant Jasnathji with 36 rules, famous for the Agni Nritya over live coals.
  • Why not (C) Ramsnehi Sect? The Ramsnehi sect was founded at Shahpura (Bhilwara) by Sant Ramcharanji, dedicated to the formless divine name Ram without idol worship.
  • Why not (D) Nishkalank Sect? The Nishkalank sect was founded at Beneshwar (Dungarpur) by Sant Mavji who authored the futuristic prophecy texts called *Chopada*.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: 29 Rules & Khejdi = Vishnoi (Jambhoji); 36 Rules & Fire Dance = Jasnathi (Jasnathji); Chopada Prophecies = Nishkalank (Mavji).
🎯 Bhakti Tradition — Dadu Dayal & Disciples — Deep Concept Check RPSC College Lecturer & EO/RO
Which disciple of Sant Dadu Dayal remained dressed in bridegroom's wedding attire (दूल्हे के वेश में) for his entire life after hearing Dadu's spiritual discourse, authoring the classic text 'Sarvangi' (सर्वांगी)?
(A) Sant Sundardas (संत सुंदरदास - Sundar Vilas)
(B) Sant Rajjabji (संत रज्जब जी)
(C) Sant Haridas (संत हरिदास - Niranjani Sect)
(D) Sant Bakhnaji (संत बखना जी)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Sant Rajjabji (संत रज्जब जी)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Sant Rajjabji (born Sanganer, Jaipur) was on his way to get married when he heard Dadu Dayal's sermon: 'रज्जब तैं गजब कियौ, सिर पर बांध्यौ मोड़। आया था हरि भजन कौं, करै नरक की दौड़॥'. He immediately renounced worldly life, stayed in bridegroom attire for the rest of his life, and compiled Dadu's teachings in *Sarvangi* and *Rajjab Vani*.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Sant Sundardas? Sant Sundardas (born Dausa) was a highly educated Brahmin disciple of Dadu who founded the Naga branch of armed ascetics and composed *Sundar Vilas* and *Gyan Samudra*.
  • Why not (C) Sant Haridas? Sant Haridas (originally bandit Hari Singh Sankhla of Kaprod, Didwana) was transformed into the 'Valmiki of Kalyug' and founded the Niranjani Sampradaya.
  • Why not (D) Sant Bakhnaji? Sant Bakhnaji of Naraina was another of the 52 chief disciples ('52 Pillars') who composed devotional padas in Dhundhari.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Groom's attire & Sarvangi = Rajjabji; Naga branch & Sundar Vilas = Sundardas; Valmiki of Kalyug = Haridas Niranjani.

# 🎯 Chapter 2: Topic-wise RPSC Previous Years' Questions (PYQs)

Q1 RPSC RAS Pre 2018 / 2nd Grade 2022
Which of the following is NOT included among the traditional "Panch Pirs" (पंच पीर) of Rajasthan?
(A) Tejaji (तेजाजी)
(B) Pabuji (पाबूजी)
(C) Gogaji (गोगाजी)
(D) Ramdevji (रामदेवजी)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Tejaji (तेजाजी)
Explanation: The classic mnemonic verse is: पाबू, हड़बू, रामदे, मांगलिया मेहा। पांचू पीर पधारज्ये, गोगाजी जेहा॥ (Pabuji, Harbhuji, Ramdevji, Mehaji Mangaliya, Gogaji). Tejaji is a legendary Lok Devta but is not categorized under the Panch Pirs.
Q2 RPSC SI 2021 / Constable 2020
Which folk deity is revered in Rajasthan as the "Protector of Camels" (ऊंटों के देवता) and the deity for curing plague?
(A) Pabuji (पाबूजी)
(B) Devnarayanji (देवनारायणजी)
(C) Mehaji (मेहाजी)
(D) Mallinathji (मल्लीनाथजी)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Pabuji (पाबूजी)
Explanation: Pabuji Rathore brought camels (Sandein) to Marwar for the first time. The Raika / Rebari community considers Pabuji their patron deity. When a camel falls sick, devotees vow to recite *Pabuji ki Phad*.
Q3 RPSC College Lecturer 2021 / RAS 2013
Sultan Mahmud of Ghaznavi conferred the title of "Jahir Pir" (जाहिर पीर - Living/Visible Saint) upon which folk deity?
(A) Harbhuji
(B) Tejaji
(C) Gogaji
(D) Ramdevji
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Gogaji
Explanation: Gogaji Chauhan fought heroically against Mahmud of Ghaznavi to liberate stolen cows. Impressed by his supernatural combat abilities, Ghaznavi proclaimed him *Jahir Pir*. His shrine is at Gogamedi (Hanumangarh).
Q4 RPSC 1st Grade 2020 / Patwari 2021
The grand state-level animal cattle fair in honor of Veer Tejaji is held annually at:
(A) Asind (Bhilwara)
(B) Kolumand (Phalodi)
(C) Kharnal (Nagaur)
(D) Parbatsar (Didwana-Kuchaman)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Parbatsar (Didwana-Kuchaman)
Explanation: Veer Tejaji was born in Kharnal, but the grand state revenue-generating cattle fair (*Veer Teja Animal Fair*) is organized at Parbatsar on Bhadrapada Shukla Dashami (Teja Dashami).
Q5 RPSC EO/RO 2022 / 2nd Grade 2022
Where is the Chhatri / Samadhi of the folk deity Kallaji Rathore (चार हाथों वाले देवता - Four-Armed Deity) located?
(A) Chittorgarh Fort
(B) Gagron Fort
(C) Mehrangarh Fort
(D) Kumbhalgarh Fort
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Chittorgarh Fort
Explanation: During the 3rd Saka of Chittorgarh (1567–68), Veer Kallaji carried his injured uncle Jaimal Rathore on his shoulders, wielding four swords at once against Akbar's forces. His cenotaph stands between Bhairav Pol and Hanuman Pol at Chittorgarh.
Q6 RPSC RAS Pre 2021 / SI 2021
Guru Jambhoji established the Bishnoi sect in 1485 AD at Samrathal Dhora by preaching how many religious rules (नियम)?
(A) 20 rules
(B) 42 rules
(C) 36 rules
(D) 29 rules
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (D) 29 rules
Explanation: 20 (Bees) + 9 (Noi) = 29 rules. These include environmental protection (never felling green Khejri trees), wildlife compassion (protecting blackbucks), and moral purity. (Note: Jasnathi sect has 36 rules; Charandasi sect has 42 rules).
Q7 RPSC RAS Pre 2018 / REET 2022
The famous 'Fire Dance' (अग्नि नृत्य) over live burning embers while chanting "Fateh-Fateh" is performed by the disciples of:
(A) Ramsnehi Sect of Shahpura
(B) Bishnoi Sect of Mukam
(C) Jasnathi Sect of Katariasar (Bikaner)
(D) Dadupanth of Naraina
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Jasnathi Sect of Katariasar (Bikaner)
Explanation: Founded by Saint Jasnathji (1482–1506 AD). The Siddha performers dance barefoot on glowing embers (*Dhoona*) in divine frenzy, tossing embers and demonstrating agricultural acts (*Matira Phodna*).
Q8 RPSC 2nd Grade 2019 / College Lecturer 2021
Which of the following Bhakti saints is renowned as the "Kabir of Rajasthan" (राजस्थान का कबीर)?
(A) Saint Raidas (संत रैदास)
(B) Saint Dadu Dayal (संत दादू दयाल)
(C) Saint Mavji (संत मावजी)
(D) Saint Pipa (संत पीपा)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Saint Dadu Dayal (संत दादू दयाल)
Explanation: Dadu Dayal (1544–1603 AD) taught Nirguna Bhakti, social casteless unity, and non-sectarian devotion (*Nipakh / निपक्ष मार्ग*). His principal seat is Naraina (Jaipur), and his 52 prominent disciples are called *Bawan Stambha* (52 स्तंभ).
Q9 RPSC RAS Pre 2021 / EO/RO 2022
The prophetic text *Chopra* (चोपड़ा ग्रंथ), containing predictions about the future in Vagadi dialect, is associated with:
(A) Saint Sundardas
(B) Saint Charandas
(C) Saint Mavji
(D) Saint Haridas
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Saint Mavji
Explanation: Saint Mavji founded the *Nishkalank Sect* at Beneshwar (Dungarpur). His 5 volumes of prophecies (*Chopra*) are brought out for public viewing exclusively on Diwali.
Q10 RPSC SI 2021 / RAS Pre 2016
The annual 'Phool Dol Mahotsav' (फूलडोल महोत्सव) celebrated from Chaitra Krishna Pratipada to Panchami is the signature festival of:
(A) Pranami Sect of Panna
(B) Ramsnehi Sect of Shahpura (Bhilwara)
(C) Nimbarka Sect of Salemabad
(D) Vallabh Sect of Nathdwara
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Ramsnehi Sect of Shahpura (Bhilwara)
Explanation: Founded by Saint Ramcharan Ji (1719–1798 AD). Followers chant "Ram" and wear pink robes (*Gulabi Bhesh*). The Phool Dol festival takes place immediately after Holi at their Ramdwara in Shahpura.

CHAPTER 3
Chapter 3: Major Festivals, Fairs and Celebrations of Rajasthan (राजस्थान के प्रमुख त्यौहार, मेले एवं उत्सव)

🎙️ Faculty Classroom Opening: The Rhythm of Rajasthani Festivals (त्यौहारों का चक्र)
"There is an old saying in Rajasthan: 'Saat Vaar, Nau Tyohar' (सात वार, नौ त्यौहार) (In seven days of the week, Rajasthan celebrates nine festivals!). When life in the desert was tough, festivals provided color, community joy, and emotional rejuvenation. But from an RPSC examiner's perspective, this is a pure calendar test. If you master the Hindu Lunar Calendar (Chaitra to Phalguna with Krishna and Shukla Pakshas / कृष्ण पक्ष व शुक्ल पक्ष), this chapter becomes your highest-scoring area."
🧠 Master Mnemonic: The Cycle of Festivals (त्यौहारों का आरम्भ व अंत)
"तीज त्यौहारां बावड़ी, ले डूबी गणगौर" (Teej Tyoharan Bawdi, Le Doobi Gangaur)
  • The annual cycle of festivals begins with Chhoti Teej (छोटी तीज) (Shravana month).
  • The annual cycle of festivals ends with Gangaur (गणगौर) (Chaitra month).

# 1. Major Traditional Festivals

# A. Teej (तीज) & Gangaur (गणगौर) 🔥 Repeated in Every Single Exam 

                                  TEEJ & GANGAUR CALENDAR (तीज व गणगौर)
                                                     │
         ┌───────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │                                           │                                           │
   CHHOTI TEEJ (छोटी तीज)                      BADI TEEJ (बड़ी तीज)                        GANGAUR (गणगौर)
(Shravana Shukla 3 / श्रावण शुक्ल 3)       (Bhadrapada Krishna 3 / भाद्रपद कृष्ण 3)       (Chaitra Shukla 1-3 / चैत्र शुक्ल 1-3)
         │                                           │                                           │
 • Celebrated in Jaipur (जयपुर)              • Celebrated in Bundi (बूंदी)              • Jaipur & Udaipur (जयपुर व उदयपुर)
 • Nature & Swings (झूला)                    • Kajali / Satudi / Boorhi Teej            • 18-day Puja of Isar-Parvati
 • Delicacy: Ghewar (घेवर)                   • (कजली / सातुड़ी / बूढ़ी तीज)              • Pink Gangaur: Nathdwara (Chaitra Shukla 5)
 • Gift: Sinjara (सिंजारा)                   • Grand royal procession in Bundi          • Without Isar: Jaisalmer (Chaitra Shukla 4)
  1. Chhoti Teej / Hariyali Teej (छोटी तीज / हरियाली तीज - Shravana Shukla Tritiya):
    • Dedicated to Goddess Parvati (पार्वती) uniting with Lord Shiva (शिव). Marks the onset of the monsoon.
    • Royal procession taken out from the City Palace in Jaipur. Special sweet: Ghewar (घेवर).
    • Day before is Sinjara (सिंजारा) (gifts of clothes, sweets, and henna sent to daughters/fiancées).
  2. Badi Teej / Kajali Teej / Satudi Teej (बड़ी तीज / कजली तीज / सातुड़ी तीज - Bhadrapada Krishna Tritiya): 🔥 Asked 6+ Times
    • Celebrated 15 days after Chhoti Teej. Women fast and worship the sacred neem (नीम) tree.
    • World-renowned grand royal procession held in Bundi (बूंदी).
  3. Gangaur (गणगौर - Chaitra Shukla Pratipada to Tritiya):
    • The festival of 18 days celebrating marital bliss. Isar (ईसर) = Lord Shiva; Gauri / Gavar (गौरी / गवर) = Goddess Parvati.
    • Royal processions in Jaipur and Udaipur (Col. Tod gave an enchanting description of Udaipur's Gangaur procession).
    • Special Regional Variations (Examiner's Favorite!):
      • Pink Gangaur / Chunari Gangaur (गुलाबी गणगौर / चुनड़ी गणगौर): Celebrated on Chaitra Shukla Panchami (चैत्र शुक्ल पंचमी) at Nathdwara (नाथद्वारा - Rajsamand).
      • Gangaur without Isar (बिना ईसर की गवर): Celebrated on Chaitra Shukla Chaturthi (चैत्र शुक्ल चतुर्थी) exclusively in Jaisalmer (जैसलमेर) (Bikaner's army snatched the Isar idol during a historical raid, so only Gauri is taken out in procession).
      • Dhinga Gavar / Bentmar Gangaur (धींगा गवर / बेंतमार गणगौर): Celebrated on Vaishakha Krishna Tritiya (वैशाख कृष्ण तृतीया) in Jodhpur (जोधपुर) (initiated by Maharana Raj Singh; women dress in fancy costumes and playfully strike men with sticks).

# B. Other Critical Hindu Festivals

  • Sheetlashtami / Basoda (शीतलाष्टमी / बासोड़ा - Chaitra Krishna Ashtami):
    • Worship of Goddess Sheetla Mata (शीतला माता) (Shrine atop Seel-ki-Doongri / शील की डूंगरी, Chaksu / चाकसू, Jaipur; priest is a Kumhar / कुम्हार, vehicle is a donkey / गधा).
    • No fire is lit in kitchens; families consume cold, stale food (Basoda / बासोड़ा) cooked the previous evening to protect from seasonal viral illnesses.
  • Akshaya Tritiya / Akha Teej (अक्षय तृतीया / आखा तीज - Vaishakha Shukla Tritiya):
    • The foremost Abujh Muhurat (अबूझ सावा) (most auspicious un-choghadiya day for mass weddings).
    • Bikaner Foundation Day (Rao Bika founded Bikaner on this day in 1488 AD); marked by massive kite-flying competitions in Bikaner and Jaipur. Seven food grains (Satnaja) and Khichda (खींचड़ा) are cooked.
  • Holi (होली - Phalguna Purnima / फाल्गुन पूर्णिमा) — Regional Varieties (RPSC Match Table):
    • Lathmar Holi (लट्ठमार होली): Shri Mahavirji (श्री महावीर जी - Karauli) (women beat men with bamboo sticks).
    • Kodamar Holi (कोड़ामार होली): Bhinai (भिणाय - Ajmer/Kekri) (leather/cloth whips).
    • Patharmar Holi (पत्थरमार होली): Barmer (बाड़मेर) (stone pelting).
    • Gobar ke Kandon ki Holi (गोबर के कण्डों की होली): Galiakot (गलियाकोट - Dungarpur) (cow-dung cakes).
    • Angaron ki Holi (अंगारों की होली): Kekri (केकड़ी) & Lalsot (लालसोट - Dausa) (walking on burning coals).
    • Devar-Bhabhi Holi (देवर-भाभी होली): Beawar (ब्यावर - Ajmer).
    • Illuji ki Savari (इलोजी की सवारी): Taken out during Holi in Barmer (Illuji is the folk deity of romance and bachelors).

# 2. Prominent Fairs of Rajasthan (राजस्थान के प्रमुख मेले) 

                                  MAJOR FAIRS OF RAJASTHAN (प्रमुख मेले)
                                                     │
         ┌───────────────────┬───────────────────────┴───────────────────────┬───────────────────┐
         │                   │                                               │                   │
    PUSHKAR FAIR      BENESHWAR FAIR                                  KARNI MATA FAIR     KAILADEVI FAIR
   (पुष्कर मेला)       (बेणेश्वर मेला)                                 (करणी माता मेला)    (कैलादेवी मेला)
 (Kartika Purnima)    (Magha Purnima)                               (Chaitra/Ashvin Nav) (Chaitra Shukla 8)
         │                   │                                               │                   │
   • Ajmer / 52 Ghats  • Dungarpur (Triveni Sangam)                  • Deshnok (Bikaner) • Karauli (Kalisil River)
   • Camel Trade       • "Vagad / Tribal Kumbh" (आदिवासियों का कुम्भ)  • "Temple of Mice"  • Languriya Songs (लांगुरिया)
   • Dipdan (दीपदान)   • Broken Shivling Worship (खण्डित शिवलिंग)     • White Mice (काबा) • Lakhi Mela (लक्खी मेला)
  • Pushkar Fair (पुष्कर मेला - Ajmer - Kartika Purnima / कार्तिक पूर्णिमा): Known as the "Rose of Rajasthan" and the most colorful fair in India. 52 bathing ghats; huge camel and cattle trade; Brahma temple worship; sacred Dipdan (दीपदान).
  • Beneshwar Fair (बेणेश्वर मेला - Dungarpur - Magha Purnima / माघ पूर्णिमा): Revered as the "Kumbh of Tribals" (आदिवासियों का महाकुम्भ) and Vagad ka Pushkar. Located at the Triveni Sangam of Som, Mahi, and Jakham rivers in Nawata-para / Sabla (नवाटापरा / साबला). Only place in the world where a broken/fragmented Shivling (खण्डित शिवलिंग) is worshipped.
  • Karni Mata Fair (करणी माता मेला - Deshnok, Bikaner / देशनोक, बीकानेर): Celebrated twice a year during Navratris. Known as the "Temple of Mice" (चूहों वाली देवी). Sacred white mice are called Kaba (काबा); sighting a Kaba is considered a direct blessing.
  • Kailadevi Fair (कैलादेवी मेला - Karauli - Chaitra Shukla Ashtami / चैत्र शुक्ल अष्टमी): Major Lakhi Mela (लक्खी मेला) on Trikoot Hill (त्रिकूट पर्वत) along River Kalisil (कालीसिल नदी). Devotees sing Languriya devotional songs (लांगुरिया गीत) and dance in front of Bohra Bhagat's Chhatri (बोहरा भगत की छतरी).
  • Sitabari Fair (सीताबाड़ी मेला - Kelwara, Baran / केलवाड़ा, बारां - Jyeshtha Amavasya / ज्येष्ठ अमावस्या): Revered as the "Kumbh of the Sahariya Tribe" (सहरिया जनजाति का कुम्भ) (associated with Goddess Sita and Maharishi Valmiki).
  • Khatushyamji Fair (खाटूश्यामजी मेला - Sikar - Phalguna Shukla Ekadashi/Dwadashi): Worship of Barbarik (बर्बरीक) as "Sheesh ke Dani" (शीश के दानी) and "Haare ka Sahara" (हारे का सहारा).
  • Urs of Khwaja Moinuddin Chishti (ख्वाजा मोईनुद्दीन चिश्ती का उर्स - Ajmer - 1st to 6th Rajab / रजब माह): Hoisting of ceremonial flag on Buland Darwaza (बुलन्द दरवाजा) by Ghori family of Bhilwara. Prasad cooked in massive cauldrons: Badi Deg (बड़ी देग) (4,800 kg - Akbar) and Chhoti Deg (छोटी देग) (2,240 kg - Jahangir).

# ⚡ Chapter 3: 1-Minute Quick Cramming Table

Festival / Fair Hindu Lunar Date (Tithi) Key Identifying Feature
