# 📝 Chapter 5: High-Yield Practice Questions (अभ्यास प्रश्न) — Expected RPSC Pattern

Practice Q1 🎯 Rajasthan Painting Schools & Characteristics
Consider the following statements regarding the Painting Styles of Rajasthan:
1. Depiction of mango trees, elephants, and yellow color dominance is a key characteristic of the Mewar School.
2. The Kishangarh style reached its zenith under Raja Sawant Singh (Nagardas), and Nihal Chand painted the celebrated portrait of 'Bani Thani'.
3. The Bundi painting style is globally famous for its animated depiction of birds, animals, lush vegetation, and monsoon skies (पशु-पक्षियों का चित्रण).
Which of the statements given above are correct?
(A) 1, 2 and 3 (All correct)
(B) 1 and 3 only
(C) 2 and 3 only
(D) 1 and 2 only
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) 1, 2 and 3 (All correct)
Explanation: All three statements represent canonical characteristics of Rajasthan painting traditions. Eric Dickinson and Dr. Fayaz Ali introduced Kishangarh's Bani Thani to the world, comparing it to Leonardo da Vinci's Mona Lisa. In 1973, the Indian Postal Department issued a postage stamp on Bani Thani.
Practice Q2 🎯 Handicrafts & GI Tag Centers
Match List-I (Handicraft / Folk Art) with List-II (Famous Production Center):
List-I (Art Form)
(A) Thewa Art (थेवा कला)
(B) Usta Art (उस्ता कला)
(C) Blue Pottery (ब्लू पॉटरी)
(D) Kavad Woodcraft (कावड़ कला)
List-II (Center)
(i) Bassi (Chittorgarh)
(ii) Pratapgarh
(iii) Bikaner
(iv) Jaipur
(A) (A)-(i), (B)-(ii), (C)-(iii), (D)-(iv)
(B) (A)-(ii), (B)-(iv), (C)-(iii), (D)-(i)
(C) (A)-(ii), (B)-(iii), (C)-(iv), (D)-(i)
(D) (A)-(iii), (B)-(ii), (C)-(i), (D)-(iv)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) (A)-(ii), (B)-(iii), (C)-(iv), (D)-(i)
Explanation:
• Thewa Art = Pratapgarh (Soni family, fine gold inlay work on colored Belgian glass).
• Usta Art = Bikaner (Hisamuddin Usta, camel hide gold embossing).
• Blue Pottery = Jaipur (Turquoise/cobalt glazing on clay-less dough).
• Kavad Art = Bassi, Chittorgarh (Prabhatji Suthar, portable wooden story shrines).
Practice Q3 🎯 Phad & Pichwai Painting Special
Which of the following statements regarding the Phad Paintings (फड़ चित्रकला) of Rajasthan is INCORRECT?
(A) Shrilal Joshi was awarded the Padma Shri for revitalizing Phad painting.
(B) Devnarayanji's Phad is the longest and oldest Phad, on which a 5-rupee postage stamp was issued in 1992.
(C) Shahpura (Bhilwara) is the primary center, maintained by the traditional Chhipa Joshi family.
(D) Pabuji's Phad is sung by Nayak Bhopas accompanied by the Jantar musical instrument.
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Pabuji's Phad is sung by Nayak Bhopas accompanied by the Jantar musical instrument.
Explanation: Pabuji's Phad is accompanied by the Ravanhattha (रावणहत्था) instrument. Jantar (जंतर) is used by Gujjar Bhopas during the narration of Devnarayanji's Phad.
Examiner Trap: Always remember: Pabuji = Ravanhattha; Devnarayanji = Jantar.
Practice Q4 🎯 Dabu & Resist Printing Centers
Match List-I (Dabu Resist Material) with List-II (Regional Center):
List-I (Dabu Type)
(A) Clay Dabu (मिट्टी का दाबू)
(B) Wax Dabu (मोम का दाबू)
(C) Wheat Bran Dabu (बींदन/गेहूं की बींदणी)
List-II (Location)
(i) Sawai Madhopur
(ii) Akola (Chittorgarh)
(iii) Bagru & Sanganer (Jaipur)
(A) (A)-(iii), (B)-(i), (C)-(ii)
(B) (A)-(i), (B)-(ii), (C)-(iii)
(C) (A)-(ii), (B)-(i), (C)-(iii)
(D) (A)-(ii), (B)-(iii), (C)-(i)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) (A)-(ii), (B)-(i), (C)-(iii)
Explanation:
• Clay/Mud Dabu = Akola (Chittorgarh) — famous for 'Dabu print of Akola'.
• Wax (Mom) Dabu = Sawai Madhopur.
• Wheat bran/Bindani Dabu = Sanganer & Bagru (Jaipur).

# 🌟 Comprehensive Topic-Wise Master Concept Questions (प्रत्येक विषय के गहन अभ्यास प्रश्न)

🎯 Painting Schools — Kishangarh School & 'Chandni Raat ri Sangeet Gosthi' — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & 1st Grade
While master painter Nihal Chand immortalized *Bani-Thani*, which other court artist of the Kishangarh School of Painting created the world-famous masterpiece titled 'Chandni Raat ri Sangeet Gosthi' (चांदनी रात की संगीत गोष्ठी - Moonlight Musical Concert)?
(A) Amar Chand (अमरचन्द)
(B) Sahibdin (साहिबदीन)
(C) Nisardin (निसारदीन)
(D) Ruknuddin (रुक्नुद्दीन)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Amar Chand (अमरचन्द)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: The celebrated painting **'Chandni Raat ri Sangeet Gosthi'** (depicting royal lovers and musicians floating under starry moonlit silver waters) was painted by master artist **Amar Chand** during the reign of Sawant Singh / Sardar Singh in Kishangarh.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B) Sahibdin? Sahibdin was the master painter of Mewar who painted *Ragamala (1628), Geet Govinda (1629), and Arsh Ramayana (1651)*.
  • Why not (C) Nisardin? Nisardin painted the *Chawand Ragamala (1605)* in Mewar.
  • Why not (D) Ruknuddin? Ruknuddin was the court master of the Bikaner School (*Usta / Rasikapriya*).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Chandni Raat ri Sangeet Gosthi = Amar Chand (Kishangarh); Bani-Thani = Nihal Chand (Kishangarh); Chawand Ragamala (1605) = Nisardin (Mewar).
🎯 Painting Schools — Devgarh Sub-school & Dr. Shridhar Andhare — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & 1st Grade
The Devgarh Sub-school of Painting (देवगढ़ उपशैली), established in 1680 AD under Rawat Dwarkadas Chundawat and blending stylistic elements of Mewar, Marwar, and Jaipur, was brought to international acclaim by which pioneering art historian?
(A) Ananda Coomaraswamy
(B) Dr. Shridhar Andhare (डॉ. श्रीधर अंधारे)
(C) Eric Dickinson
(D) Dr. Fayaz Ali
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Dr. Shridhar Andhare (डॉ. श्रीधर अंधारे)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: **Dr. Shridhar Andhare** discovered and brought the **Devgarh School of Painting** into the global spotlight. Famous court painters of Devgarh were **Bagta (बगता), Chokha (चोखा), and Kavala (कवला)**. The school is renowned for royal hunting scenes, court durbars, and yellow/green hues.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Ananda Coomaraswamy? Coomaraswamy wrote *'Rajput Painting'* in 1916 (first scientific classification of Rajasthani paintings).
  • Why not (C) Eric Dickinson? Eric Dickinson (along with Dr. Fayaz Ali) brought Kishangarh's **Bani-Thani** to international acclaim, dubbing it the *'Indian Mona Lisa'*.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Devgarh School discovered by = Dr. Shridhar Andhare (Painters: Bagta & Chokha); Bani-Thani brought to light by = Eric Dickinson & Dr. Fayaz Ali.
🎯 Painting Schools — Kota School (Women Hunting Scenes) — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & 1st Grade
Which distinct painting school of Rajasthan is world-renowned for its thrilling depiction of 'Shikar Drishya' (शिकार के दृश्य), uniquely showing royal queens and princesses actively hunting wild beasts with firearms and arrows?
(A) Bundi School (बूंदी शैली - Birds, animals and lush monsoon landscape)
(B) Amer-Jaipur School (जयपुर शैली - Life-size portraits by Sahibram)
(C) Kota School (कोटा शैली - Hunting scenes featuring women & queens)
(D) Bikaner School (बीकानेर शैली - Usta & Matheran art)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Kota School (कोटा शैली - Hunting scenes featuring women & queens)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: The **Kota School of Painting (कोटा चित्रकला शैली)** (developed under Maharao Ram Singh, Ummed Singh, and Durjanshal) specialized in dense forest wildlife and **hunting scenes (शिकार के दृश्य)** where royal women, queens, and court maidens are depicted perched on tree machans shooting tigers, leopards, and wild boars.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Bundi School? Bundi School is famous as the *'Bird & Animal Painting School'* (*पशु-पक्षियों की चित्रशैली*) with famous *Chitrashala* frescoes.
  • Why not (B) Jaipur School? Jaipur School is famous for life-size royal portraits (*Aadamkad Chitra*) painted by master Sahibram.
  • Why not (D) Bikaner School? Bikaner School is renowned for Muslim Usta painters signing their names and dates (*Usta & Matheran Kala*).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Women hunting tigers = Kota School; Animals/Birds & Chitrashala = Bundi School; Life-size portraits = Jaipur (Sahibram); Signed paintings with dates = Bikaner.
🎯 Painting Schools — Mewar School Earliest Manuscripts — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & 1st Grade
Which is the earliest dated illustrated palm-leaf manuscript of the Mewar School of Painting, executed in 1261 AD (VS 1318) at Ahar during the reign of Guhil ruler Rawal Tej Singh by painter Kamal Chandra (कमलचन्द्र)?
(A) Supasanaha Chariyam (सुपासनाह चरियम - 1423 AD)
(B) Arsh Ramayana (आर्ष रामायण - 1651 AD)
(C) Chawand Ragamala (चावण्ड रागमाला - 1605 AD)
(D) Shravaka Pratikramana Sutra Churni (श्रावक प्रतिक्रमण सूत्र चूर्णी - 1261 AD)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Shravaka Pratikramana Sutra Churni (श्रावक प्रतिक्रमण सूत्र चूर्णी - 1261 AD)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: **'Shravaka Pratikramana Sutra Churni' (1261 AD)** is the oldest illustrated manuscript of Mewar painting, inscribed on palm leaf by Jain scribe **Kamal Chandra** during Rawal Tej Singh's reign at Aghatpur (Ahar).

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Supasanaha Chariyam? *Supasanaha Chariyam* (1423 AD) was illustrated at Delwara (Mewar) under Maharana Mokal by artist Hiranand.
  • Why not (B) Arsh Ramayana? *Arsh Ramayana* (1651 AD) was painted by master Sahibdin under Maharana Jagat Singh I.
  • Why not (C) Chawand Ragamala? *Chawand Ragamala* (1605 AD) was painted by Nisardin at Chawand under Maharana Amar Singh I.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Oldest Mewar Manuscript (1261 AD) = Shravaka Pratikramana Sutra Churni (Kamal Chandra under Tej Singh); Chawand Ragamala (1605) = Nisardin (Amar Singh I).
🎯 Folk Arts — Thewa Art of Pratapgarh & Nathuji Soni — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & EO/RO
The internationally acclaimed Thewa Art (थेवा कला), involving microscopic 24-carat pure gold wire filigree fused upon vibrant green Belgian glass, is the ancestral secret craft of which family in Pratapgarh?
(A) Soni Family of Pratapgarh (सोनी / राजसोनी परिवार - Founded by Nathuji Soni)
(B) Joshi Family of Shahpura (जोशी परिवार - Phad Painting)
(C) Suthar Family of Bassi (सुथार परिवार - Kavad Woodcraft)
(D) Kumhar Family of Molela (कुम्हार परिवार - Terracotta)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Soni Family of Pratapgarh (सोनी / राजसोनी परिवार - Founded by Nathuji Soni)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Thewa Art was pioneered in the late 18th century by master goldsmith **Nathuji Soni** under the royal patronage of Maharawat Samant Singh of Pratapgarh. The Soni family (honored with the title *Rajsoni* and multiple Padma Shri / National awards including Mahesh Raj Soni) keeps the exact heat-fusion gold-on-green-glass technique closely guarded, teaching it only to daughters-in-law.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B) Joshi Family? The Joshi family of Shahpura specializes in traditional canvas **Phad Painting** (Shrilal Joshi, Pratik Joshi).
  • Why not (C) Suthar Family? The Suthar family of Bassi (Chittorgarh) crafts wooden **Kavads, Torans, and Gangaur idols**.
  • Why not (D) Kumhar Family? The Kumhar family of Molela (Nathdwara) sculpts clay **Terracotta plaques** (Mohanlal Kumhar).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Thewa Art (Gold on green glass) = Pratapgarh (Nathuji Soni family); Phad = Shahpura (Joshi family); Kavad = Bassi (Suthar family); Molela = Terracotta (Kumhar family).
🎯 Painting Schools — Kishangarh & Bani Thani — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & 1st Grade
The world-famous painting 'Bani-Thani' (बणी-ठणी) of the Kishangarh School, created by master artist Nihal Chand during the reign of Sawant Singh (Nagardas), is distinguished by which iconic nose ornament?
(A) Besari (बेशरी - delicate pendant nose ornament)
(B) Chump (चूंम्प)
(C) Rakkan (रखन)
(D) Memand (मेमंद)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Besari (बेशरी - delicate pendant nose ornament)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Bani-Thani (the singer-poetess and consort of King Sawant Singh) is portrayed with arched lotus eyes, pointed chin, transparent odhni, and the signature hanging nose-ring **'Besari' (बेशरी)**. Art critic Eric Dickinson christened it the *'Mona Lisa of India'*, and the Government of India issued a 20-paise postage stamp on it on 5 May 1973.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B) Chump? Chump is a gold stud fixed inside teeth.
  • Why not (C) Rakkan? Rakkan is a metallic tooth plate.
  • Why not (D) Memand? Memand is a forehead hair ornament.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Bani Thani nose ring = Besari; Painter = Nihal Chand; Ruler = Sawant Singh (Nagardas); Postage Stamp = 1973; Title = Mona Lisa of India.
🎯 Folk Handicrafts — Blue Pottery & Padma Shri Kripal Singh — Deep Fact Check RPSC EO/RO & Assistant Professor
Blue Pottery of Jaipur (जयपुर की ब्लू पॉटरी), originally brought from Turan/Persia to Amer by Raja Man Singh I and patronized by Sawai Ram Singh II, was revolutionized by which Padma Shri artist who introduced 25 distinct new color shades (Kripal Kumbh)?
(A) Hisamuddin Usta (हिमुद्दीन उस्ता - Bikaner Usta Art)
(B) Kripal Singh Shekhawat (कृपाल सिंह शेखावत - Mau, Sikar)
(C) Mohanlal Kumhar (मोहनलाल कुम्हार - Molela Terracotta)
(D) Prabhatji Suthar (प्रभातजी सुथार - Bassi Kavad)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Kripal Singh Shekhawat (कृपाल सिंह शेखावत - Mau, Sikar)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Padma Shri **Kripal Singh Shekhawat** (born Mau village, Sikar) transformed Jaipur Blue Pottery by breaking beyond traditional turquoise blue and cobalt green to invent 25 new mineral glaze color pigments, founding his signature style *'Kripal Kumbh'*. Blue pottery uses quartz powder, glass powder, Sajji, Katira gond, and Multani mitti without using clay.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Hisamuddin Usta? Padma Shri Hisamuddin Usta of Bikaner was the master of *Usta Kala* (camel leather gold embossing).
  • Why not (C) Mohanlal Kumhar? Padma Shri Mohanlal Kumhar of Molela (Nathdwara) was the master of terracotta clay votive plaques.
  • Why not (D) Prabhatji Suthar? Prabhatji Suthar of Bassi (Chittorgarh) is the pioneer of wooden *Kavad* and *Toran* carving.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Blue Pottery & 25 colors = Kripal Singh Shekhawat; Usta Art = Hisamuddin Usta; Molela Terracotta = Mohanlal Kumhar; Kavad = Bassi (Prabhatji Suthar).
🎯 Folk Arts — Wooden Kavad & Phad Painting — Deep Fact Check RPSC 2nd Grade & SI
What is the traditional portable wooden shrine with multiple painted hinged doors unfolding religious episodes called in Rajasthani folk culture, and which town is its primary production center?
(A) Sanjhi (सांझी) — Nathdwara
(B) Pichwai (पिछवाई) — Kota
(C) Kavad (कावड़ - The Traveling Temple) — Bassi (Chittorgarh)
(D) Thewa (थेवा) — Pratapgarh
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Kavad (कावड़ - The Traveling Temple) — Bassi (Chittorgarh)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Kavad (कावड़) is a red-painted portable wooden box shrine featuring multiple hinged panels/doors painted with scenes from the Ramayana, Mahabharata, and folk deities. As the Kavadia Bhat narrates the ballad, he opens the doors one by one. **Bassi (Chittorgarh)** is the exclusive manufacturing center, pioneered by the Suthar artisan community.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Sanjhi? *Sanjhi* is the folk art of wall designs made by unmarried girls during Pitru Paksha (Shradh); banana Sanjhi is famous at Nathdwara.
  • Why not (B) Pichwai? *Pichwai* is the sacred cloth backdrop painting of Nathdwara.
  • Why not (D) Thewa? *Thewa* is gold-on-glass jewelry from Pratapgarh.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Portable wooden temple with doors = Kavad (Bassi, Chittor); Cloth scroll = Phad (Shahpura); Backdrop cloth = Pichwai (Nathdwara); Gold on glass = Thewa (Pratapgarh).
🎯 Painting Schools — Bundi & Animal-Bird Depictions — Deep Concept Check RPSC College Lecturer & RAS
Which painting sub-school of Rajasthan is renowned as the 'Bird and Animal Painting School' (पशु-पक्षियों की चित्रशैली) and features the celebrated 'Chitrashala' (चित्रशाला) built during the reign of Maharao Ummed Singh?
(A) Kota School (कोटा शैली - Hunting scenes)
(B) Bundi School (बूंदी शैली - Nature & Birds)
(C) Kishangarh School (किशनगढ़ शैली - Bani Thani)
(D) Mewar School (मेवाड़ शैली - Ragamala & Geet Govind)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Bundi School (बूंदी शैली - Nature & Birds)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: The **Bundi School of Painting** (under the Hadoti school) is world-famous for its depiction of dense forests, rains, dancing peacocks, and lush wildlife. Maharao Ummed Singh built the immortal **Chitrashala (चित्रशाला - Palace of Frescoes)** inside Bundi Fort.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Kota School? The Kota School is famous for **Hunting Scenes (शिकार के दृश्य)** where royal women and queens are depicted hunting wild animals alongside men.
  • Why not (C) Kishangarh School? The Kishangarh School is famous for Radha-Krishna romance, lotus-filled lakes, and the legendary portrait of **Bani-Thani** by Nihal Chand.
  • Why not (D) Mewar School? The Mewar School is the foundational primitive school of Rajasthan art, renowned for religious epics like *Ragamala*, *Geet Govind*, and *Arsh Ramayana* by Sahibdin and Manohar.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Birds, animals & Chitrashala = Bundi; Female hunting scenes = Kota; Bani Thani & Besari = Kishangarh; Religious epics = Mewar.
🎯 Folk Handicrafts — Thewa Art of Pratapgarh — Deep Concept Check RPSC EO/RO & Patwari
The exquisite handicraft of Thewa Art (थेवा कला), which involves intricate micro-engraving of 24-carat pure gold leaf on green Belgian glass, is the closely guarded hereditary monopoly of which artisan family in Rajasthan?
(A) Chhipa Joshi Family of Shahpura (शाहपुरा का जोशी परिवार - Phad Painting)
(B) Rajsoni / Soni Family of Pratapgarh (प्रतापगढ़ का राजसोनी परिवार)
(C) Usta Artisan Family of Bikaner (बीकानेर का उस्ता परिवार - Camel hide art)
(D) Mohanlal Kumhar Family of Molela (मोलेला का कुम्हार परिवार - Terracotta)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Rajsoni / Soni Family of Pratapgarh (प्रतापगढ़ का राजसोनी परिवार)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Thewa Art was pioneered during the reign of Maharawat Samant Singh by **Nathuji Soni (नाथूजी सोनी)** in Pratapgarh. The Soni family received the royal title 'Rajsoni' and keeps the secret technique of fusing gold onto Belgian glass strictly within the family.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Joshi Family? The Joshi family of Shahpura (Bhilwara) is world-famous for **Phad Painting (फड़ चित्रकला)**, practiced by master artists Shrilal Joshi and Pratik Joshi.
  • Why not (C) Usta Family? The Usta artisan family of Bikaner is world-famous for **Usta Kala (उस्ता कला / मुनब्बती कला)** (gold embossing on camel leather), pioneered by Padma Shri Hisamuddin Usta.
  • Why not (D) Molela Family? The Kumhar artisans of Molela (Nathdwara, Rajsamand), notably Padma Shri Mohanlal Kumhar, are famous for **Molela Terracotta (मोलेला टेराकोटा)** clay plaques of Devnarayanji and Dharmraj.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Thewa Art = Pratapgarh (Nathuji Soni); Phad Painting = Shahpura (Joshi family); Usta Art = Bikaner (Hisamuddin Usta); Terracotta = Molela (Mohanlal Kumhar).

# 🎯 Chapter 5: Topic-wise RPSC Previous Years' Questions (PYQs)

Q1 RPSC RAS Pre 2016, 2021 / EO/RO 2022
The world-famous miniature painting 'Bani-Thani' (बणी-ठणी), termed the "Mona Lisa of India" by Eric Dickinson, was painted by Nihal Chand under the patron ruler:
(A) Raja Man Singh of Amer
(B) Sawant Singh (Nagardas) of Kishangarh
(C) Maharana Jagat Singh of Mewar
(D) Maharaja Jaswant Singh of Marwar
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Sawant Singh (Nagardas) of Kishangarh
Explanation: Painted by Nihal Chand during Raja Sawant Singh's reign (1748–1764). The portrait depicts Radha with elongated arched eyebrows, pointed nose, lotus-like curved eyes (*Kamal Nayan*), and a transparent veil (*Odhni*). Indian Post issued a postage stamp on it on 5 May 1973.
Q2 RPSC EO/RO 2022
'Ladli Das' (लाडली दास) was a renowned master painter belonging to which painting style?
(A) Alwar Style
(B) Bundi Style
(C) Kishangarh Style
(D) Jaisalmer Style
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Kishangarh Style
Explanation: Prominent painters of the Kishangarh school include Nihal Chand, Amar Chand, Sitaram, Nanakram, and Ladli Das.
Q3 RPSC 1st Grade 2020 / RAS Pre 2018
Who was the court painter of Jaipur during Sawai Ishwari Singh's reign who created famous life-size portraits (आदमकद चित्र)?
(A) Muhammad Shah
(B) Ruknuddin
(C) Sahibram (साहिबराम)
(D) Shaligram
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Sahibram (साहिबराम)
Explanation: Sahibram specialized in life-size royal portraiture in the *Suratkhana* of Jaipur. (Note: Sahibdin was an artist of Mewar; Sahibram was an artist of Jaipur!).
Q4 RPSC SI 2021 / 2nd Grade 2022
'Pichhwai' (पिछवाई) painting style is the unique cultural heritage of which place in Rajasthan?
(A) Nathdwara (Rajsamand)
(B) Shahpura (Bhilwara)
(C) Pratapgarh
(D) Devgarh (Rajsamand)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Nathdwara (Rajsamand)
Explanation: Pichhwai refers to devotional cloth tapestries painted with Lord Krishna's Bal Leelas, cows, and lotus motifs hung behind the idol of Shrinathji. Female painters: Kamla and Ilaichi.
Q5 RPSC RAS Pre 2013, 2021
'Thewa Art' (थेवा कला) — the micro-engraving of fine 24-carat gold wire on colored Belgian glass — is exclusively practiced by the Rajsoni family of:
(A) Bikaner
(B) Chittorgarh
(C) Barmer
(D) Pratapgarh
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Pratapgarh
Explanation: Founded by Nathuji Soni during the reign of Maharawat Sawant Singh. The craft is an internationally patented GI-tagged secret art passed from father to son in the Rajsoni family.
Q6 RPSC SI 2021 / Patwari 2021
'Usta Kala' (उस्ता कला / मुनव्वती कला), the intricate gold embossed engraving on camel leather, flourished in which royal court?
(A) Bikaner
(B) Jodhpur
(C) Jaipur
(D) Jaisalmer
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Bikaner
Explanation: Brought to Bikaner from Lahore by Maharaja Anup Singh. Master artist Padma Shri Hisamuddin Usta popularized this craft worldwide. The Camel Hide Training Centre operates in Bikaner.
Q7 RPSC College Lecturer 2021 / RAS Pre 2018
Which Jaipur ruler originally introduced Blue Pottery (ब्लू पॉटरी) to Rajasthan from Persia/Lahore, and which master artisan was awarded the Padma Shri for reviving it with 25 distinct color shades?
(A) Madho Singh I & Kripal Singh
(B) Sawai Jai Singh & Mohanlal Kumhar
(C) Sawai Ram Singh II & Hisamuddin Usta
(D) Man Singh I & Kripal Singh Shekhawat
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Man Singh I & Kripal Singh Shekhawat
Explanation: Raja Man Singh I brought artisans from Turfan/Persia. Maharaja Sawai Ram Singh II sent potters to Delhi to learn the technique, and Padma Shri Kripal Singh Shekhawat (Mau, Sikar) developed 25 distinct new color shades (*Kripal Kumbh*).
Q8 RPSC 2nd Grade 2022
The traditional religious wall painting craft known as 'Matheran Kala' (मथेरण कला) is associated with which community of Bikaner?
(A) Jain artists of Bikaner
(B) Meghwals of Barmer
(C) Sonis of Pratapgarh
(D) Muslim Chhipas of Akola
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Jain artists of Bikaner
Explanation: Matheran art involves plaster-relief frescoes on havelis, temples, and wooden boards depicting Jain Tirthankaras and Hindu deities by the Matheran Jain community. Famous artists: Munna Lal, Mukund.

CHAPTER 6
Chapter 6: Architecture of Rajasthan — Forts, Palaces, Temples & Stepwells (राजस्थान का स्थापत्य — दुर्ग, महल, मंदिर एवं बावड़ियां)

🎙️ Faculty Classroom Opening: The Anatomy of a Rajasthani Fort (दुर्गों की संरचना)
"In Sukra Niti (शुक्र नीति), a kingdom is compared to a human body, and forts are called its Arms (भुजाएं). In Rajasthan, a fort was not just a military barracks; it was a self-sustaining fortified city containing royal palaces, granaries holding years of food, deep stepwells, weapon foundries, temples, and artisan quarters. That is why enemies had to lay siege for 6 to 12 months just to starve a fort out!"
📖 Terminology Decoder: Traditional Architectural Terms (स्थापत्य शब्दावली)
  • Pol (पोल / दरवाजा): Massive fortified entry gate fitted with iron spikes to prevent battering rams/war elephants from breaching (e.g., *Suraj Pol / सूरज पोल, Bhairon Pol / भैरव पोल, Laxman Pol / लक्ष्मण पोल*).
  • Burj (बुर्ज): Semicircular bastion projecting outward from the ramparts allowing archers and musketeers to shoot sideways at attackers.
  • Jharokha (झरोखा): Overhanging enclosed stone balcony with latticework (*Jali / जाली*) allowing royal women to view courtyards while observing Purdah.
  • Baradari (बारादरी): Open marble pavilion with 12 arched doorways designed for cooling breezes by lakes (e.g., at Anasagar Lake).
  • Baori / Vav (बावड़ी / वाव): Architectural stepwell with artistic stone stairs descending to the groundwater level.

# 1. The 6 UNESCO World Heritage Hill Forts (2013) (यूनेस्को विश्व धरोहर के 6 पहाड़ी दुर्ग) 🔥 Inscribed at Phnom Penh

🧠 Master Mnemonic: The Fruit Basket (चीकू - गाजर - आम)
"चीकू - गाजर - आम" (Chikoo - Gajar - Aam)
  • Chi (चि): Chittorgarh Fort (चित्तौड़गढ़ दुर्ग)
  • Ku (कु): Kumbhalgarh Fort (कुम्भलगढ़ दुर्ग)
  • Ga (गा): Gagron Fort (गागरोन दुर्ग - Jhalawar)
  • Ja (ज): Jaisalmer Fort (जैसलमेर दुर्ग - Sonar Qila / सोनार किला)
  • R (र): Ranthambore Fort (रणथंभौर दुर्ग - Sawai Madhopur)
  • Aam (आम): Amer Fort (आमेर दुर्ग - Jaipur)

# 2. Comprehensive Fort Profiles & History

# A. Chittorgarh Fort (चित्तौड़गढ़ दुर्ग) — "The King of Forts" (दुर्गों का सिरमौर)

  • Superlative: "गढ़ तो चित्तौड़गढ़, बाकी सब गढ़ैया" (Largest living fort in India, spanning $8\text{ km} \times 2\text{ km}$ atop the Mesha Plateau / मेसा का पठार at the confluence of Gambhiri (गम्भीरी) and Berach (बेड़च) rivers). Built by Mauryan King Chitrangad Mori (चित्रांगद मोरी).
  • Seven Main Gates (Pols / सात पोल): Padan Pol (पाडन पोल), Bhairon Pol (भैरव पोल), Hanuman Pol (हनुमान पोल), Ganesh Pol (गणेश पोल), Jorla Pol (जोड़ला पोल), Laxman Pol (लक्ष्मण पोल), Ram Pol (राम पोल).
  • Three Legendary Sakas (चित्तौड़ के तीन साके):
    1. 1303 AD: Alauddin Khilji vs Rawal Ratan Singh (Gora & Badal; Rani Padmini Jauhar).
    2. 1535 AD: Bahadur Shah of Gujarat vs Vikramaditya (Rawat Bagh Singh of Deoliya / रावत बाघ सिंह; Rani Karmavati / कर्मावती Jauhar).
    3. 1567–68 AD: Akbar vs Maharana Uday Singh (Jaimal Rathore / जयमल राठौड़ & Patta Sisodia / फत्ता सिसोदिया; Phool Kanwar / फूल कंवर Jauhar).
  • Vijay Stambh (विजय स्तम्भ - Victory Pillar): 9 stories (122 feet high, 157 stairs); built by Maharana Kumbha (1440–1448) to celebrate the Battle of Sarangpur (1437). Chief Architect: Jaita (जैता) and his sons Napa, Poma, Punja (नापा, पोमा, पूंजा). Emblem of Rajasthan Police (राजस्थान पुलिस) and Board of Secondary Education (माध्यमिक शिक्षा बोर्ड - RBSE).
  • Kirti Stambh (कीर्ति स्तम्भ - Jain Tower of Fame): 7 stories (75 feet high); built in 12th century by Jain merchant Jija Bhagerwala (जीजा शाह बघेरवाल); dedicated to 1st Tirthankara Lord Adinath (आदिनाथ).

# B. Kumbhalgarh Fort (कुम्भलगढ़ दुर्ग - Rajsamand) — "The Invincible Bastion"

  • Builder & Architect: Built by Maharana Kumbha (1448–1458) atop the Aravallis; designed by chief architect Mandan (मण्डन).
  • The Great Wall of India (भारत की महान दीवार): The perimeter fortification wall is 36 km long and so wide that 4 to 8 horsemen can ride abreast on top (second longest continuous wall in the world after the Great Wall of China).
  • Katargarh (कटारगढ़ - The Eye of Mewar): The upper inner citadel perched at the highest apex where Kumbha resided; birthplace of Maharana Pratap (9 May 1540). Abul Fazl wrote: "यह दुर्ग इतनी बुलन्दी पर बना है कि नीचे से ऊपर देखने पर सिर से पगड़ी गिर जाती है!" (It is perched at such an astonishing height that looking up at it knocks the turban off one’s head!).

# C. Gagron Fort (गागरोन दुर्ग - Jhalawar) — Premier Water Fort (जल दुर्ग) 🔥 Repeated PYQ

  • Built without foundations directly on a solid natural rock at the sacred confluence of Ahu (आहू) and Kali Sindh (कालीसिंध) rivers.
  • Contains the Dargah of Sufi Saint Meethe Shah (मीठे शाह / हमीदुद्दीन चिश्ती) and the penance cave/memorial of Saint Pipa (संत पीपा की छतरी).
  • Two Sakas (दो साके): 1423 AD (Achaldas Khinchi / अचलदास खींची vs Hoshang Shah) and 1444 AD (Mahmud Khilji I renamed it Mustafabad / मुस्तफाबाद).

# D. Ranthambore Fort (रणथंभौर दुर्ग - Sawai Madhopur) — The Armored Fort (बख्तरबंद दुर्ग)

  • Surrounded by 7 concentric forested hills. Historian Abul Fazl famously remarked: "अन्य सब दुर्ग नंगे हैं, जबकि यह दुर्ग बख्तरबंद है!" (All other forts in Hindustan are bare, but this fort is armored).
  • Houses the famous Trinetra Ganesh Temple (त्रिनेत्र गणेश मन्दिर) and the 32-pillared cenotaph (Battis Khambhon ki Chhatri / बत्तीस खम्भों की छतरी / Nyay ki Chhatri / न्याय की छतरी built by Hammir).
  • 1st Saka of Rajasthan (1301 AD): Hammir Dev Chauhan vs Alauddin Khilji (Rani Rangdevi led Jauhar).

# E. Mehrangarh Fort (मेहरानगढ़ दुर्ग - Jodhpur) — The Citadel of the Sun

  • Built by Rao Jodha in 1459 AD on Chidiya-tunk hill (चिड़ियाटूंंक पहाड़ी); known as Mayurdhwajgarh (मयूरध्वजगढ़) (peacock-shaped).
  • Rudyard Kipling (रुडयार्ड किपलिंग) exclaimed: "A palace that might have been built by Titans and colored by the morning sun... the work of fairies and giants!" (परियों और देवताओं द्वारा निर्मित).
  • Famous Cannons on Ramparts (तोपें): Kilkila (किलकिला), Shambhuban (शंभूबाण), Gajni Khan (गजनी खां), Kadak Bijli (कड़क बिजली).

# F. Lohagarh Fort (लोहागढ़ दुर्ग - Bharatpur) — The Invincible Mud Fort (अजय मिट्टी का किला)

  • Constructed in 1733 AD by Maharaja Surajmal (महाराजा सूरजमल - Plato of Jats / जाटों का प्लेटो).
  • Engineered with a double defensive wall: inner stone wall surrounded by an outer 8-meter-thick mud rampart, encircled by a deep moat (Sujan Ganga Canal / सुजान गंगा नहर fed by River Banganga / Moti Jheel).
  • In 1805, British Commander Lord Lake (लॉर्ड लेक) attacked Lohagarh 5 consecutive times with heavy artillery but failed to breach the shock-absorbing mud ramparts!

# 3. Masterpieces of Temples, Stepwells & Royal Cenotaphs

  • Dilwara Jain Temples (देलवाड़ा के जैन मन्दिर - Mount Abu / माउंट आबू):
    • Vimal Vasahi Temple (विमल वसही - 1031 AD): Dedicated to 1st Tirthankara Rishabhdev (built by Vimal Shah / विमल शाह, architect: Kirtidhar).
    • Luna Vasahi Temple (लूण वसही - 1230 AD): Dedicated to 22nd Tirthankara Neminath (built by brothers Vastupala & Tejapala / वास्तुपाल व तेजपाल; features Devrani-Jethani ka Gokhra / देवरानी-जेठानी का गोखड़ा).
  • Ranakpur Jain Temple (रणकपुर जैन मन्दिर - Pali / पाली): Dedicated to Lord Adinath; built by Dharanashah (धरणक शाह) during Rana Kumbha's reign (architect: Depak / देपाक). Famous worldwide for its 1,444 uniquely carved marble pillars (1444 खम्भों का वन) (no two pillars are identical!).
  • Chand Baori (चाँद बावड़ी - Abhaneri, Dausa / आभानेरी, दौसा): World's deepest and most visually stunning stepwell (built in 8th century by King Chanda / राजा चन्द in Gurjara-Pratihara style); 13 stories deep with 3,500 maze-like symmetrical stone steps opposite Harshat Mata Temple (हर्षत माता मन्दिर).
  • Rani-ji ki Baori (रानी जी की बावड़ी - Bundi): Acclaimed as the "Queen of Stepwells" (बावड़ियों का सिरमौर); built in 1699 AD by Rani Nathavati (रानी नाथावती).
  • Major Royal Cenotaphs (प्रमुख छतरियां):
    • 84-Pillared Cenotaph (चौरासी खम्भों की छतरी): Bundi (Rao Anirudh Singh for foster-brother Dhabhai Deva).
    • 80-Pillared Cenotaph / Moosi Maharani (अस्सी खम्भों की छतरी / मूसी महारानी): Alwar (Maharaja Vinay Singh).
    • Jaswant Thada (जसवंत थड़ा): Jodhpur (White marble cenotaph built by Maharaja Sardar Singh for Jaswant Singh II; called the "Taj Mahal of Marwar" / मारवाड़ का ताजमहल).

# ⚡ Chapter 6: 1-Minute Quick Cramming Table

# 📝 Chapter 6: High-Yield Practice Questions (अभ्यास प्रश्न) — Expected RPSC Pattern

Practice Q1 🎯 UNESCO Heritage & Fort Architecture
Which of the following hill forts of Rajasthan was NOT included in the prestigious UNESCO World Heritage List in 2013 (Phnom Penh 37th Session)?
(A) Kumbhalgarh Fort (Rajsamand)
(B) Jaisalmer Fort (Sonar Qila)
(C) Gagron Fort (Jhalawar)
(D) Mehrangarh Fort (Jodhpur)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Mehrangarh Fort (Jodhpur)
Explanation: The 6 UNESCO Hill Forts of Rajasthan are remembered by the famous mnemonic "चिकू गाजर आम" (CHIKU GAJAR AAM):
• चि = Chittorgarh
• कु = Kumbhalgarh
• गा = Gagron
• ज = Jaisalmer
• र = Ranthambhore
• आम = Amer
Mehrangarh and Junagarh are NOT in the UNESCO list.
Practice Q2 🎯 Stepwells & Historical Baoris
The world-renowned Chand Baori (चांद बावड़ी), known for its intricate geometrical maze of 3,500 narrow steps and adjacent Harshat Mata temple, is located at:
(A) Neemrana (Kotputli-Behror)
(B) Abhaneri (Dausa)
(C) Osian (Jodhpur)
(D) Bundi
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Abhaneri (Dausa)
Explanation: Chand Baori at Abhaneri (Dausa) was built in the 8th-9th century by King Chandra (Nikumbha Rajput) in the Gurjara-Pratihara style. It is one of the deepest and most visually stunning stepwells in the world.
Practice Q3 🎯 Water Fort & Unlaid Foundations
Which fort of Rajasthan is classified as a prime example of an Audak Durg (Jal Durg / Water Fort), resting directly upon bare rocky foundations without mortar or excavated foundation trenches at the confluence of the Ahu and Kali Sindh rivers?
(A) Bhainsrorgarh Fort (Chittorgarh)
(B) Shergarh Fort (Baran)
(C) Gagron Fort (Jhalawar)
(D) Manohar Thana Fort (Jhalawar)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Gagron Fort (Jhalawar)
Explanation: Gagron Fort (Jhalawar) stands on a solid rocky ridge encircled by Ahu and Kali Sindh rivers on three sides. It has no foundation trench (बिना नींव का दुर्ग). It is famous for Achaldas Khichi's heroic saka in 1423 CE against Hoshang Shah of Malwa, and houses the Dargah of Sufi saint Mithe Shah (Hamiduddin).
Practice Q4 🎯 Chhatris & Cenotaphs Architecture
Match List-I (Historic Chhatri / Cenotaph) with List-II (Location):
List-I (Chhatri)
(A) 84-Pillared Chhatri (चौरासी खंभों की छतरी)
(B) 80-Pillared Chhatri / Moosi Maharani Chhatri
(C) 32-Pillared Chhatri of Hammir Chauhan
(D) 16-Pillared Chhatri of Amar Singh Rathore
List-II (Location)
(i) Alwar
(ii) Bundi
(iii) Nagaur Fort
(iv) Ranthambhore Fort
(A) (A)-(ii), (B)-(iv), (C)-(i), (D)-(iii)
(B) (A)-(iv), (B)-(i), (C)-(ii), (D)-(iii)
(C) (A)-(ii), (B)-(i), (C)-(iv), (D)-(iii)
(D) (A)-(i), (B)-(ii), (C)-(iii), (D)-(iv)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) (A)-(ii), (B)-(i), (C)-(iv), (D)-(iii)
Explanation:
• 84-Pillared Chhatri = Bundi (Built by Rao Raja Aniruddh Singh in memory of foster brother Dhaabhai Deva).
• 80-Pillared Chhatri = Alwar (Moosi Maharani & Maharaja Bakhtawar Singh, built by Vinay Singh).
• 32-Pillared Chhatri = Ranthambhore (Built by Hammir Dev Chauhan for his father Jaitra Singh's 32-year rule).
• 16-Pillared Chhatri = Nagaur Fort (Amar Singh Rathore).

# 🌟 Comprehensive Topic-Wise Master Concept Questions (प्रत्येक विषय के गहन अभ्यास प्रश्न)

🎯 Forts Architecture — Gagron Fort (Ahu & Kali Sindh Confluence) — Deep Fact Check RPSC 1st Grade & RAS Pre
Which magnificent hill-cum-water fort (Jal Durg / Audak Durg) of Rajasthan stands directly on solid rock without any foundation trenches at the confluence of the Ahu and Kali Sindh rivers in Jhalawar, and houses the Dargah of Sufi Saint Mithe Shah (Dewan Shah)?
(A) Bhaisrodrgarh Fort (Chittor - Chambal & Bamani)
(B) Shergarh Fort (Dholpur - Chambal)
(C) Gagron Fort (गागरोन दुर्ग, झालावाड़ - UNESCO World Heritage Fort)
(D) Kanor Fort
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Gagron Fort (गागरोन दुर्ग, झालावाड़ - UNESCO World Heritage Fort)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: **Gagron Fort (Jhalawar)** is India's prime specimen of an **Audak / Jal Durg (जल दुर्ग)**. Surrounded on 3 sides by the waters of the **Ahu and Kali Sindh**, it was constructed by Dod Rajputs (Bijaldeva) and later ruled by Khinchi Chauhans (Achaldas Khinchi). It contains the tomb of **Sant Pipa**, Dargah of **Mithe Shah**, and the colossal **Buland Darwaza** built by Aurangzeb.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Bhaisrodrgarh? Bhaisrodrgarh (Chittorgarh) is located at the confluence of the **Chambal and Bamani** rivers ('Vellore of Rajasthan').
  • Why not (B) Shergarh? Shergarh is located on the Chambal river in Dholpur (houses the colossal *Hunhunkar cannon*).
  • Why not (D) Kanor? Kanor is in Udaipur.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Jal Durg at Ahu + Kali Sindh = Gagron Fort (Jhalawar); Jal Durg at Chambal + Bamani = Bhaisrodrgarh (Chittor).
🎯 Havelis of Rajasthan — Nathmal ki Haveli (Jaisalmer) — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & 2nd Grade
Which world-famous 19th-century haveli in Jaisalmer, adorned with life-size yellow stone elephants (named *Lalu* and *Hathi*) at the entrance, was carved simultaneously from left and right halves by two architect brothers named Hathi and Lalu without using any drawn blueprints?
(A) Patwon ki Haveli (पटवों की हवेली - 5 Storeys)
(B) Rampuria Haveli (बीकानेर)
(C) Salim Singh ki Haveli (सालिम सिंह की हवेली - 9 Storeys / Moti Mahal)
(D) Nathmal ki Haveli (नथमल की हवेली, जैसलमेर)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Nathmal ki Haveli (नथमल की हवेली, जैसलमेर)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Built in 1885 for Diwan Nathmal, **Nathmal ki Haveli** was carved by two master architect brothers, **Hathi and Lalu**. Working independently on two separate halves (left and right) without a unified plan, they achieved near-perfect visual symmetry with miniature stone carvings of trains, bicycles, and floral lace.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Patwon ki Haveli? Patwon ki Haveli is a cluster of 5 mansions built by Guman Chand Patwa for his 5 sons.
  • Why not (B) Rampuria Haveli? Rampuria Havelis are located in Bikaner crafted in red Dulmera sandstone.
  • Why not (C) Salim Singh ki Haveli? Salim Singh ki Haveli (9 storeys) has a top floor designed like a peacock-shaped glass ship (*Jahaz Mahal / Moti Mahal*).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Architect brothers Hathi & Lalu = Nathmal Haveli; 5 Mansions for 5 sons = Patwon Haveli; 9 Storeys ship shape = Salim Singh Haveli (All in Jaisalmer).
🎯 Forts Architecture — Kumbhalgarh & Abul Fazl's Quote — Deep Fact Check RPSC 1st Grade & RAS Pre
Regarding which formidable hill-citadel of Rajasthan did Mughal historian Abul Fazl write in the Akbarnama: 'यह दुर्ग इतनी बुलन्दी पर बना हुआ है कि नीचे से ऊपर देखने पर सिर से पगड़ी गिर जाती है'?
(A) Chittorgarh Fort (चित्तौड़गढ़ दुर्ग)
(B) Kumbhalgarh Fort — Katargarh (कुम्भलगढ़ दुर्ग / कटारगढ़)
(C) Mehrangarh Fort (मेहरानगढ़ दुर्ग)
(D) Ranthambore Fort (रणथम्भौर दुर्ग)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Kumbhalgarh Fort — Katargarh (कुम्भलगढ़ दुर्ग / कटारगढ़)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Abul Fazl made this famous observation about the inner citadel **Katargarh (कटारगढ़ - Eye of Mewar)** inside **Kumbhalgarh Fort (Rajsamand)**. Designed by architect **Mandan (मण्डन)** for Maharana Kumbha (1448–1458 AD), Kumbhalgarh stands on 13 mountain peaks and possesses a **36-km-long defense wall** ('The Great Wall of India'). It is also the birthplace of Maharana Pratap (*Badal Mahal / Juna Kacheri*).

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Chittorgarh? Chittorgarh is praised as *'Garh to Chittorgarh, Baki sab Garhaiya'* situated on the flat Mesha plateau.
  • Why not (C) Mehrangarh? Rudyard Kipling described Mehrangarh as *'Built by angels, fairies and giants'*.
  • Why not (D) Ranthambore? Abul Fazl described Ranthambore as *'All other forts are bare, but this fort is armored (बख्तरबंद)'*.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Pugree falls when looking up = Kumbhalgarh (Katargarh); Armored fort (बख्तरबंद) = Ranthambore; Built by angels/fairies = Mehrangarh.
🎯 Forts Architecture — Ranthambore & Nyaya ki Chhatri — Deep Fact Check RPSC EO/RO & 2nd Grade
The 32-Pillared Cenotaph (32 खम्भों की छतरी) inside Ranthambore Fort, constructed by Rana Hammir Dev Chauhan to commemorate the 32-year reign of his father Jaitra Singh, is popularly known as which of the following?
(A) Chaurasi Khambhon ki Chhatri (84 Pillars - Bundi)
(B) Moosi Maharani ki Chhatri (80 Pillars - Alwar)
(C) Nyaya ki Chhatri / Umbrella of Justice (न्याय की छतरी)
(D) Gora-Badal ki Chhatri (Chittor)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Nyaya ki Chhatri / Umbrella of Justice (न्याय की छतरी)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Rana Hammir Dev built the magnificent red stone **32-Pillared Cenotaph** inside Ranthambore Fort in honor of his father Jaitra Singh's 32-year rule. Hammir sat beneath this grand domed pavilion to deliver royal judicial verdicts to his subjects, earning it the immortal name **'Nyaya ki Chhatri' (न्याय की छतरी)**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) 84 Pillars? The 84-Pillared Cenotaph (*Chaurasi Khambhon ki Chhatri*) is located in **Bundi**, built by Rao Raja Anirudh Singh for foster brother Deva.
  • Why not (B) Moosi Maharani? The 80-Pillared Cenotaph of *Moosi Maharani* is located in **Alwar**, built by Maharaja Vinay Singh in 1815.
  • Why not (D) Gora-Badal? Gora-Badal's dome is located inside Chittorgarh Fort.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Nyaya ki Chhatri (32 pillars) = Ranthambore (Hammir for father Jaitra Singh); 84 Pillars = Bundi; 80 Pillars = Alwar (Moosi Maharani).
🎯 Temples Architecture — Kiradu 'Khajuraho of Rajasthan' — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & RAS Pre
Which magnificent cluster of 5 Hindu temples in Hathma village (Barmer), built in the 11th–12th century Gurjara-Pratihara / Mahu-Maru style and featuring the grand Someshwara Temple, is celebrated as the 'Khajuraho of Rajasthan' (राजस्थान का खजुराहो)?
(A) Bhanddevra Temple (भांडदेवरा - Ramgarh, Baran)
(B) Harshat Mata Temple (हर्षत माता मंदिर - Abhaneri)
(C) Jagat Ambika Temple (जगत अम्बिका मंदिर - Udaipur)
(D) Kiradu Temples (किराडू के मंदिर, बाड़मेर)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Kiradu Temples (किराडू के मंदिर, बाड़मेर)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: The **Kiradu Temples (किराडू)** near Barmer are universally recognized as the **'Khajuraho of Rajasthan'** for their intricate erotic and mythological stone sculptures depicting life, war, and spiritual cosmos. The largest and most ornate temple is dedicated to **Lord Someshwara (Shiva)**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Bhanddevra? Bhanddevra (Baran) is celebrated as the **'Khajuraho of Hadoti'** or *'Mini Khajuraho'*.
  • Why not (B) Harshat Mata? Harshat Mata is an 8th-century Pratihara temple in Abhaneri (Dausa) adjacent to Chand Baori.
  • Why not (C) Jagat Ambika? Jagat Ambika (Udaipur) is celebrated as the **'Khajuraho of Mewar'**.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Khajuraho of Rajasthan = Kiradu (Barmer); Khajuraho of Mewar = Jagat Ambika (Udaipur); Khajuraho of Hadoti = Bhanddevra (Baran).
🎯 Forts Architecture — Gagron Water Fort (Ahu & Kali Sindh) — Deep Fact Check RPSC College Lecturer & RAS Pre
Why is Gagron Fort (गागरोन दुर्ग, झालावाड़) classified as the premier Jal Durg / Audak Durg (जल दुर्ग / औदक दुर्ग) of Rajasthan?
(A) Because it has a massive underground swimming pool built by Akbar
(B) Because it was built by the British Navy as a naval dockyard
(C) Because it is situated in the middle of Lake Pichola
(D) Because it is surrounded on three sides by the deep waters of the Ahu and Kali Sindh rivers and rests directly on a solid rock cliff without any artificial foundation
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Because it is surrounded on three sides by the deep waters of the Ahu and Kali Sindh rivers and rests directly on a solid rock cliff without any artificial foundation
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Gagron Fort (built by King Bijaldev Doda in the 12th century and ruled by Khinchi Chauhans like Achaldas Khinchi) is situated at the confluence (*Sangam*) of the **Ahu and Kali Sindh rivers** in Jhalawar. It is constructed directly on natural stone without foundation trenches, houses the tomb of Sufi saint **Mitheshah (Hamiduddin Chishti)**, and witnessed 2 historic Sakas (1423 AD & 1444 AD).

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A)? Akbar did not build Gagron; he granted it to Prithviraj Rathore of Bikaner (who composed *Veli Krishan Rukmani ri* here).
  • Why not (B)? Gagron is a medieval Rajput fort, not a colonial dockyard.
  • Why not (C)? Lake Pichola in Udaipur contains the *Jag Niwas (Lake Palace)* and *Jag Mandir*, not Gagron.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Premier Jal Durg = Gagron (Ahu + Kali Sindh); Author of Veli Krishan Rukmani ri at Gagron = Prithviraj Rathore; Tomb of Mitheshah = Gagron.
🎯 Temples Architecture — Ranakpur Chaumukha Jain Temple — Deep Fact Check RPSC RAS Pre & 1st Grade
The architectural wonder Ranakpur Chaumukha Jain Temple (रणकपुर जैन मंदिर, पाली), built in 1439 AD during the reign of Maharana Kumbha on the banks of the Maghai River, is dedicated to which Tirthankara and features how many intricately carved stone pillars?
(A) Lord Rishabhdev / Adinath (भगवान आदिनाथ); 1,444 pillars (no two pillars have identical carving)
(B) Lord Mahavira; 84 pillars
(C) Lord Parshvanatha; 108 pillars
(D) Lord Neminatha; 500 pillars
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Lord Rishabhdev / Adinath (भगवान आदिनाथ); 1,444 pillars (no two pillars have identical carving)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Ranakpur Temple was commissioned by Jain merchant **Dharnak Shah (धरणक शाह)** and designed by master architect **Depak (देपाक)** in 1439 AD under Maharana Kumbha. It is dedicated to the 1st Jain Tirthankara **Adinath / Rishabhdev (भगवान आदिनाथ)** and is renowned as the *'Forest of Pillars'* (*स्तम्भों का वन*) with **1,444 unique sculpted marble pillars**.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (B)? Lord Mahavira's premier temple is at Shri Mahavirji (Karauli); 84 refers to the 84-pillared cenotaph (*Chhatri*) of Bundi.
  • Why not (C)? Parshvanatha temples exist at Lodhruva and Nakoda; 108 is a common prayer bead count.
  • Why not (D)? Neminatha's famous Luna Vasahi temple is at Dilwara (Mount Abu).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Ranakpur = 1st Tirthankara Adinath + 1,444 Pillars + Architect Depak + Dharnak Shah (under Kumbha) on Maghai river.
🎯 Hill Forts — Kumbhalgarh & Katargarh — Deep Concept Check RPSC RAS Pre & 1st Grade
Which innermost citadel of Kumbhalgarh Fort was the personal residence of Maharana Kumbha and was described by Abul Fazl: 'This fort is built at such a tremendous height that when one looks up from below, the turban falls off one's head'?
(A) Vijay Stambh (विजय स्तम्भ - Chittorgarh)
(B) Katargarh (कटारगढ़ - "Eye of Mewar")
(C) Supari Mahal (सुपारी महल - Ranthambore)
(D) Chokhelao Mahal (चोखेलाव महल - Mehrangarh)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Katargarh (कटारगढ़ - "Eye of Mewar")
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: Katargarh is the highest innermost fort (citadel) of Kumbhalgarh, serving as Maharana Kumbha's private sanctuary and administrative viewpoint (hence nicknamed the *'Eye of Mewar'*). Maharana Pratap was born here on 9 May 1540 in the Badal Mahal.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Vijay Stambh? Vijay Stambh is the 9-story victory monument inside Chittorgarh Fort built by Kumbha to celebrate the 1437 Battle of Sarangpur.
  • Why not (C) Supari Mahal? Supari Mahal is a historic palace inside Ranthambore Fort that uniquely houses a Hindu temple, Islamic mosque, and Christian church within the same complex.
  • Why not (D) Chokhelao Mahal? Chokhelao Mahal is a 16th-century palace inside Mehrangarh Fort (Jodhpur) built by Rao Maldeo, decorated with Ramayana-themed battle frescoes.

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Turban falls off looking up = Katargarh (Kumbhalgarh); Supari Mahal (Temple-Mosque-Church) = Ranthambore; Chokhelao Mahal = Mehrangarh.
🎯 Temple Architecture — Gurjara-Pratihara Style — Deep Concept Check RPSC College Lecturer & EO/RO
The Someshwara Temple of Kiradu (किराडू का सोमेश्वर मंदिर, बाड़मेर), hailed as the 'Khajuraho of Rajasthan' (राजस्थान का खजुराहो), is a sublime architectural masterpiece built in which classical Indian temple style?
(A) Dravidian Style (द्रविड़ शैली - South Indian Vimana)
(B) Gurjara-Pratihara / Mahu-Maru Style (गुर्जर-प्रतिहार / महा-मारू शैली)
(C) Bhumija Style (भूमिज शैली - Udayeshwara style)
(D) Indo-Saracenic Style (इंडो-सारसेनिक शैली)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Detailed 4-Option Analysis
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Gurjara-Pratihara / Mahu-Maru Style (गुर्जर-प्रतिहार / महा-मारू शैली)
🎯 Why this is the Correct Answer: The Kiradu group of 5 temples (dating to the 11th–12th centuries under Parmar feudatories) represents the pinnacle of the **Gurjara-Pratihara / Mahu-Maru style** in western Rajasthan, especially the Someshwara Shiva Temple with its erotic sculptures and ornate pillars.

🔍 Comparative Distractor Breakdown (अन्य विकल्पों का विस्तृत विश्लेषण — Why Not the Others?):
  • Why not (A) Dravidian Style? Dravidian style originated in South India; in Rajasthan, the Rangji Temple in Pushkar is built in the Dravidian Gopuram architectural style.
  • Why not (C) Bhumija Style? Bhumija is a sub-style of Nagara architecture characterized by miniature decorative spirelets (*Urushringas*); prime examples in Rajasthan are the **Mahanaleshwar Temple at Menal** and **Undeshwar Temple at Bijolia**.
  • Why not (D) Indo-Saracenic? Indo-Saracenic is the late 19th-century colonial blend of British, Rajput, and Mughal architecture seen in Albert Hall Museum (Jaipur) and Umaid Bhawan (Jodhpur).

💡 RPSC Memory Hook / Trap Alert: Kiradu (Khajuraho of Raj) = Gurjara-Pratihara / Mahu-Maru; Pushkar Rangji = Dravidian; Menal Mahanaleshwar = Bhumija; Albert Hall = Indo-Saracenic.

# 🎯 Chapter 6: Topic-wise RPSC Previous Years' Questions (PYQs)

Q1 RPSC RAS Pre 2016 / EO/RO 2022
Which six hill forts of Rajasthan were inscribed on the UNESCO World Heritage list in 2013?
(A) Kumbhalgarh, Siwana, Jalore, Amer, Gagron, Lohagarh
(B) Chittorgarh, Kumbhalgarh, Ranthambore, Gagron, Amer, Jaisalmer
(C) Ranthambore, Jaigarh, Nahargarh, Amer, Chittor, Gagron
(D) Chittorgarh, Mehrangarh, Taragarh, Amer, Jaisalmer, Junagarh
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (B) Chittorgarh, Kumbhalgarh, Ranthambore, Gagron, Amer, Jaisalmer
Explanation: In June 2013 at Phnom Penh (Cambodia), 6 hill forts were inscribed.
Mnemonic: चीकू गाजर आम (Chikoo-Gajar-Aam) -> Chi (Chittorgarh), Ku (Kumbhalgarh), Ga (Gagron), Jar (Jaisalmer), R (Ranthambore), Aam (Amer).
Q2 RPSC SI 2021 / 1st Grade 2022
'Gagron Fort' (गागरोन दुर्ग - Jhalawar) is the finest prototype of which classification of fort (Durg) in Rajasthan?
(A) Giri Durg (गिरि दुर्ग)
(B) Vana Durg (वन दुर्ग)
(C) Dhanvan Durg (धान्वन दुर्ग)
(D) Jal Durg / Audak Durg (जल/औदक दुर्ग)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Jal Durg / Audak Durg (जल/औदक दुर्ग)
Explanation: Gagron Fort is surrounded on three sides by the confluence of the **Ahu and Kali Sindh** rivers in Jhalawar. It stands directly on a natural rock ridge without foundation masonry.
Q3 RPSC 2nd Grade 2022 / Patwari 2021
Which fort has a 36-km-long defense wall (second longest in the world) and contains the inner citadel 'Katargarh' (कटारगढ़ - The Eye of Mewar)?
(A) Achalgarh Fort
(B) Mehrangarh Fort
(C) Chittorgarh Fort
(D) Kumbhalgarh Fort
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (D) Kumbhalgarh Fort
Explanation: Built by Maharana Kumbha on the designs of royal architect Mandan. The upper citadel *Katargarh* was Kumbha's personal residence and the birthplace of Maharana Pratap. Abul Fazl wrote: "Looking up at this fort causes the turban to fall off one's head."
Q4 RPSC College Lecturer 2021 / RAS Pre 2015
'Lalgarh Palace' (लालगढ़ पैलेस) of Bikaner was constructed in red sandstone by Maharaja Ganga Singh in memory of:
(A) His father Maharaja Lal Singh
(B) Maharaja Surat Singh
(C) Rao Bika
(D) Maharaja Anup Singh
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) His father Maharaja Lal Singh
Explanation: Built between 1902–1926 by Maharaja Ganga Singh in memory of his father Maharaja Lal Singh. Designed by British architect Sir Samuel Swinton Jacob in Indo-Saracenic style.
Q5 RPSC EO/RO 2022
Which of the following temple(s) is/are located in Amer? (A) Jagat Shiromani Temple, (B) Ambikeshwar Mahadev Temple, (C) Raktdantika Mata Temple
(A) Only (B)
(B) (A) and (B)
(C) Only (A)
(D) (A), (B) and (C)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (B) (A) and (B)
Explanation: Jagat Shiromani Temple was built by Rani Kanakwati (enshrining the Krishna idol worshipped by Meera Bai). Ambikeshwar Mahadev is the ancestral shrine from which Amer derives its name. Raktdantika Mata is situated in Satur, Bundi.
Q6 RPSC RAS Pre 2021 / SI 2021
'Rani ji ki Baori' (रानी जी की बावड़ी) in Bundi — hailed as the "Queen of Stepwells" — was built in 1699 AD by:
(A) Rani Padmini
(B) Rani Karmavati
(C) Rani Nathavati (रानी नाथावती)
(D) Rani Kanakwati
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Rani Nathavati (रानी नाथावती)
Explanation: Rani Nathavati, wife of Rao Raja Anirudh Singh and mother of Maharao Raja Budh Singh, built this ornate 3-storeyed stepwell in Bundi (the "City of Stepwells").
Q7 RPSC 2nd Grade 2022 / Constable 2020
The breathtaking 8th-century stepwell 'Chand Baori' (चांद बावड़ी) featuring 3,500 geometric steps is located in:
(A) Abhaneri (Dausa)
(B) Osian (Jodhpur)
(C) Neemrana (Kotputli-Behror)
(D) Todaraisingh (Tonk)
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (A) Abhaneri (Dausa)
Explanation: Built by King Chandra of the Nikumbha dynasty adjacent to the Harshat Mata temple in Abhaneri (Dausa). It is 13 storeys deep.
Q8 RPSC Assistant Professor 2021
Which fort constructed atop 'Cheel ka Tola' (Eagle's Hill) in Jaipur housed the cannon foundry and Asia's largest cannon-on-wheels, 'Jaivana' (जयबाण तोप)?
(A) Nahargarh Fort
(B) Bala Qila
(C) Jaigarh Fort
(D) Amer Fort
👁️ Click to View Answer & Explanation
✅ Correct Answer: (C) Jaigarh Fort
Explanation: Built by Sawai Jai Singh II as a military fortress with a canon foundry (*Topkhana*). Jaivana cannon has a barrel length of 20 feet and was test-fired only once, creating the Golelao pond in Chaksu.

CHAPTER 7
Chapter 7: Folk Music, Folk Dances and Folk Drama of Rajasthan (राजस्थान के लोक संगीत, लोक नृत्य एवं लोक नाट्य)

🎙️ Faculty Classroom Opening: The Melodic Deserts (मरुधरा का संगीत)
"When the searing hot Loo winds blow across Jaisalmer and Barmer, life seems quiet. But as dusk falls and the desert cools, the haunting, resonant notes of the Kamayacha (कामायचा) and Sarangi (सारंगी) rise from the dunes. Folk singing in Rajasthan was preserved by hereditary musician castes—the Langas (लांगा), the Manganiyars (मांगणियार), the Dhadhis (ढाढ़ी), and the Kalbeliyas (कालबेलिया). Their songs are not mere entertainment; they are living oral archives of medieval history, romance, separated lovers, and desert ecology."

# 1. Classical Gharanas & Traditional Folk Singing

  • Kathak Dance (कथक नृत्य - Jaipur Gharana / जयपुर घराना): Pioneer: Bhanuji Maharaj (भानुजी महाराज); known for high-speed pirouettes (Chakkars), complex footwork (Tatkar), and Bhav (classical dance originated from Rajasthan temples).
  • Jaipur-Atrauli Gharana (जयपुर-अतरौली घराना): Founded by Ustad Alladiya Khan (उस्ताद अल्लादिया खां) (exponents: Kishori Amonkar, Kesarbai Kerkar).
  • Maand Singing (मांड गायकी) & Official State Song:
    • "Kesariya Balam Aao Ni Padharo Mhare Desh" (केसरिया बालम आवो नी पधारो म्हारे देस): Official State Song of Rajasthan (inviting the beloved/traveler to the motherland).
    • Allah Jilai Bai (अल्लाह जिलाई बाई - Bikaner): Immortalized Kesariya Balam; awarded Padma Shri in 1982 and Royal Albert Hall Court Singer award. Other famous Maand singers: Gavari Devi (गवरी देवी - Jodhpur/Pali) and Banno Begum (बन्नो बेगम - Jaipur).
  • Famous Folk Songs (RPSC High-Frequency Identifiers):
    • Kurjan (कूरजां): Heartbreaking Viraha (separation / विरह गीत) song where a yearning wife sends love messages to her distant husband via the migratory Siberian crane (Kurjan / कूरजां पक्षी).
    • Gorband (गोरबंद): Rhythmic celebration of stitching the ornamental neck harness of a camel (ऊंट के गले का आभूषण).
    • Suvatiya (सुवटिया): Bhil woman's longing song sending a message through a parrot (Suva / तोता).
    • Hichki (हिचकी): Folk song of Mewat (Alwar) sung when remembering someone far away.
    • Kamadh (कामण): Sung to protect a bridegroom from black magic and the evil eye (जादू-टोने से बचाव).

# 2. Folk Dances of Rajasthan (राजस्थान के लोक नृत्य) 

                                  FOLK DANCES OF RAJASTHAN (लोक नृत्य)
                                                     │
         ┌───────────────────┬───────────────────────┴───────────────────────┬───────────────────┐
         │                   │                                               │                   │
    STATE / HERITAGE     TRIBAL DANCES (जनजातीय नृत्य)                   PROFESSIONAL        REGIONAL DANCES
   (राज्य / शास्त्रीय)       │                                           (व्यावसायिक नृत्य)    (क्षेत्रीय नृत्य)
         │                   │                                               │                   │
 • Ghoomar (घूमर)         • Bhil: Gair, Gavri, Hathimana                  • Terah Taali       • Bam Dance (बमरसिया - Alwar/Brt)
 • Kalbeliya (कालबेलिया - • Garasia: Valar (वालर - No Inst.)              • Agni Dance        • Dhol Dance (ढोल - Jalore - Thaakna)
   UNESCO 2010)          • Sahariya: Shikari (शिकारी)                    • Chari Dance       • Gidar & Kachhi Ghodi (गीदड़ व
                         • Kanjar: Chakri, Dhakad (चकरी, धाकड़)           • Bhavai Dance        कच्छी घोड़ी - Shekhawati)

# Detailed Dance Profiles

  • Ghoomar (घूमर): 🔥 State Dance of Rajasthan
    • Revered as the "Soul of Folk Dances" (Lok Nrityon ki Atma / लोक नृत्यों की आत्मा) and the Sirmour of Dances (नृत्यों का सिरमौर).
    • Graceful circular pirouettes of women wearing 80-kali skirts; set to an 8-beat rhythm called Sawaai (सवाई).
  • Kalbeliya Dance (कालबेलिया नृत्य): 🔥 UNESCO Heritage (2010)
    • Sensuous snake-dance performed to Pungi (पूंजी/बीन) and Khanjari (खंजरी) by the nomadic snake-charmer community; globally popularized by Padma Shri Gulabo Sapera (गुलाबो सपेरा).
    • 4 Sub-Dances: Indoni (इण्डोणी), Shankariya (शंकरिया - romantic couple dance), Panihari (पणिहारी), Bagariya (बागड़िया - performed while begging).
  • Terah Taali Dance (तेरह ताली नृत्य - Ramdevra):
    • Performed exclusively by seated Kamad (कामुड़) women in devotion to Ramdevji.
    • 13 brass cymbals (Manjiras / मंजीरे) tied on the body (9 on right leg, 2 on elbows, 2 held in hands); maestro: Mangi Bai (मांगी बाई).
  • Valar Dance (वालर नृत्य - Garasia Tribe / गरासिया जनजाति): 🔥 RPSC Favorite
    • Slow, graceful circular dance performed without any musical instruments whatsoever (बिना किसी वाद्य यंत्र के) (dancers keep rhythm through hand-claps and foot-tapping).
  • Gavri / Rai Dance-Drama (गवरी / राई नृत्य - Bhil Tribe / भील जनजाति):
    • 40-day sacred ritual dance-drama performed exclusively by Bhil men in Bhadrapada, reenacting the conflict between Lord Shiva and demon Bhasmasur (शिव-भस्मासुर कथा).
  • Agni Dance (अग्नि नृत्य - Katariasar, Bikaner): Performed barefoot over live embers by Jasnathi Siddhs chanting "Fateh Fateh" (फतेह-फतेह).
  • Chari Dance (चरी नृत्य - Kishangarh): Gujjar women dance balancing 7 brass pots with flaming cottonseeds on their heads (master: Phalku Bai / फलकू बाई).
  • Bhavai Dance (भवाई नृत्य): Acrobatic dance balancing 8 to 10 earthen pots on the head while dancing on naked sword blades, broken glass, and brass plate edges (Roop Singh Shekhawat / रूपसिंह शेखावत, Dayaram / दयाराम).

# 3. Folk Drama of Rajasthan (राजस्थान के लोक नाट्य)

  • Turra-Kalangi Khayal (तुर्रा-कलंगी ख्याल - Mewar / Chittor): 🔥 Communal Harmony
    • Created by Hindu saint Tukangir (तुकनगीर) (Turra / Shiva) and Muslim saint Shah Ali (शाह अली) (Kalangi / Parvati). Poetic duels (Dangal / दंगल) staged on grand Akhadas (अखाड़े).
  • Kuchamani Khayal (कुचामणी ख्याल - Nagaur): Pioneered by Pt. Lachhiram (पं. लक्षीराम); operatic singing with open-air stages.
  • Rammat (रम्मत - Bikaner & Jaisalmer): Staged on wooden community platforms (Patta Sanskriti / पाटा संस्कृति). In 1943, Tej Kavi of Jaisalmer (तेज कवि जैसलमेरी) authored the revolutionary play Swatantra Bawni (स्वतंत्र बावनी), openly challenging British rule and presenting the book to Mahatma Gandhi.
  • Tamasha (तमाशा - Jaipur): Open-air musical theater started under Sawai Pratap Singh by Banshidhar Bhatt (बंशीधर भट्ट).
  • Chaar-Bait (चारबैंत - Tonk): 300-year-old Pashtun musical poetry performed with a tambourine (Dhaf / डफ) in Tonk.

# ⚡ Chapter 7: 1-Minute Quick Cramming Table

Dance / Drama / Song Distinctive Core Feature Key Region / Pioneer Exponent
Ghoomar (घूमर) State Dance / 8-beat Sawaai rhythm Rajasthan Statewide / Royal Court
Kalbeliya (कालबेलिया) UNESCO 2010 / Pungi & Khanjari Gulabo Sapera / Nomadic Tribes
Terah Taali (तेरह ताली) 13 Manjiras tied on body (Kamad women) Ramdevra / Mangi Bai
